### [1] [Incremental Live Programming via Shortcut Memoization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19560)
*Marisa Kirisame,Thomas J. Porter,Ruqing Yang,Jianqiu Zhao,Yudi Wu,Ivan Wei,Cyrus Omar,Pavel Panchekha*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Chordata是一个基于快捷记忆化的增量程序解释器，通过观察程序先前版本的执行来学习重复计算模式，并在重新执行相同或结构相似的程序片段时应用这些快捷方式，显著提升实时编程系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时编程系统需要在程序编辑时快速显示动态影响，但重新执行大型或复杂程序面临计算挑战。现有系统需要增量式程序解释器实现，以解决性能瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出Chordata增量程序解释器，基于快捷记忆化技术。该方法：1) 为具有基于重写语义的任何语言提供形式化语义；2) 将Hazel实时编程系统表达为CEK机器；3) 开发实用启发式方法学习高价值快捷方式；4) 通过观察先前程序版本的执行学习重复计算模式。

Result: 在学生解决简单编程问题的编辑轨迹上评估：1) 相比基线实现13.03倍加速；2) 内存开销为19.97倍；3) 对于较小更改和更复杂程序，加速效果更显著；4) 在单个执行内也能提供加速，输入越大加速越快。

Conclusion: Chordata通过快捷记忆化有效实现了增量程序解释，显著提升实时编程系统性能，特别是在程序编辑频繁的场景下。形式化语义和实用启发式方法确保了正确性和实用性。

Abstract: Live programming systems aim to quickly show programmers the dynamic impacts of program edits. To do so, they re-execute the program whenever it is edited, which poses a computational challenge when programs become large or complex. This has led to the need for incrementality in the implementation of live program interpreters. This paper introduces Chordata, an incremental program interpreter based on shortcut memoization, which learns repeated patterns of computation, called shortcuts, by observing executions of previous versions of a program. It can then apply these shortcuts when the same or a structurally similar program fragment is re-executed. This paper contributes a formal semantics of shortcut memoization for any language with a rewrite-based semantics, with mechanized proofs of key correctness properties. We then express a variant of the Hazel live programming system, expressed as a CEK machine, in Chordata, and develop a number of practical heuristics to learn high-value shortcuts. We evaluate the resulting system on editing traces of students solving simple programming problems. Chordata achieves a speedup of 13.03\times compared to baseline with a 19.97\times memory overhead. For smaller changes and for more complex programs, Chordata achieves even greater speedups. Furthermore, we show that Chordata is capable of providing a speedup even within a single execution, with a faster speedup on a larger input.

</details>


### [2] [Is It a Good Idea to Build an HLS Tool on Top of MLIR? Experience from Building the Dynamatic HLS Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19856)
*Jiahui Xu,Emmet Murphy,Lana Josipovic*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文基于Dynamatic开发经验，评估MLIR是否真正解决了HLS社区在LLVM中遇到的问题，以及它是否是HLS的"正确"/"最佳"编译器基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIR项目最初承诺解决HLS社区在LLVM项目中遇到的问题，但作者希望通过Dynamatic项目的开发经验，验证MLIR是否真正兑现了这一承诺，并评估它是否确实是HLS领域最合适的编译器基础设施。

Method: 基于Dynamatic项目的实际开发经验进行分析，Dynamatic是一个开源项目（github.com/EPFL-LAP/dynamatic），通过具体实践来评估MLIR在HLS领域的适用性。

Result: 论文未提供具体结果，但暗示将通过Dynamatic项目的开发经验来分享对MLIR在HLS领域适用性的评估和见解。

Conclusion: 作者将基于Dynamatic项目的实践经验，分享对MLIR是否真正解决HLS社区问题以及是否是最佳编译器基础设施的评估结论。

Abstract: When the MLIR project was first introduced, it promised to address the issues that the HLS community had with the LLVM project. But is this really the case, and is MLIR the "right"/"best" compiler infrastructure for HLS? We here share our experiences based on the development of Dynamatic (github.com/EPFL-LAP/dynamatic).

</details>


### [3] [Sound State Encodings in Translational Separation Logic Verifiers (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20001)
*Hongyi Ling,Thibault Dardinier,Ellen Arlt,Peter Müller*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出了首个用于证明具有非平凡状态编码的翻译式分离逻辑验证器正确性的形式化框架，解决了现有方法需要前后端状态模型相同的强假设问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有翻译式验证器的形式化方法基于分离逻辑，但仅支持在强假设下证明前端翻译的正确性——要求输入程序和中间验证语言（IVL）具有相同的状态模型。这一假设在实践中不现实，特别是因为状态模型也定义了支持的分离逻辑资源。

Method: 提出了一个形式化框架，仅假设前端和IVL状态模型之间存在同态编码关系。核心是一个新颖的条件——向后可满足性，这对保证前端翻译的正确性至关重要。框架针对基于并发分离逻辑的前端验证器和分离逻辑IVL（如Raven、VeriFast、Viper）进行了形式化。

Result: 通过证明三种常见状态编码的正确性展示了框架的表达能力。所有框架和证明都在Isabelle/HOL中进行了形式化。

Conclusion: 这是首个能够处理非平凡状态编码的翻译式分离逻辑验证器正确性证明的形式化框架，解决了现有方法的不现实假设问题，为实际验证器提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Automated program verifiers are often organized into a front-end, which encodes an input program into an intermediate verification language (IVL), and a back-end, which proves that the IVL program is correct. Soundness of such translational verifiers requires that the back-end verification is sound and that correctness of the IVL program implies correctness of the input program. Existing formalizations for translational verifiers based on separation logic target the former, but support the latter only under the strong assumption that there exists a separation logic for the input program with the same state model as the IVL. This assumption is unrealistic in practice, especially since the state model also defines the supported separation logic resources.
  We present the first formal framework for proving the soundness of translational separation logic verifiers with non-trivial state encodings. To be applicable to various front-ends and IVLs, our framework only assumes the existence of a homomorphic encoding relation between the front-end and IVL state models. At the core of our framework is a novel condition, backward satisfiability, which is crucial to guarantee the soundness of the front-end translation. We formalize our framework for front-end verifiers based on concurrent separation logic and separation logic IVLs, such as Raven, VeriFast, and Viper. We demonstrate its expressiveness by proving soundness for three common state encodings. Our framework and all proofs are formalized in Isabelle/HOL.

</details>


### [4] [Analyzing Decoders for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20127)
*Abtin Molavi,Feras Saad,Aws Albarghouthi*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于评估量子纠错解码器的新系统分析方法，替代传统的蒙特卡洛模拟方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错解码器的准确性直接影响量子计算的规模，现有基于蒙特卡洛模拟的评估方法存在样本需求大、方差高等缺点

Method: 开发了QEC程序核心语言的形式语义，基于Stim电路格式；结合结构化错误空间搜索和约束多项式优化内核来量化解码器准确性和鲁棒性

Result: 该方法在低错误率区域表现优于模拟方法，能够量化解码器在物理错误率区间内的鲁棒性

Conclusion: 为容错量子系统设计提供了理论基础，提供了一种比传统模拟更可靠的解码器评估方法

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) enables reliable computation on noisy hardware by encoding logical information across many physical qubits and periodically measuring parities to detect errors. A decoder is the classical algorithm that uses these measurements to infer which error most likely occurred, so that the system can correct it. The decoder's accuracy-how rarely it makes the wrong guess-directly determines the scale of quantum computation that can be reliably executed. With a wealth of competing decoding algorithms, a QEC system designer needs reliable methods to evaluate them. Today, the dominant approach is to evaluate decoders using Monte Carlo simulation. However, simulation has several drawbacks such as requiring many samples to produce low variance estimates.
  In this work, we develop a new systematic analysis for evaluating decoders. We introduce a novel formal semantics of a core language for QEC programs that captures the de facto standard Stim circuit format, providing a principled theoretical foundation for the emerging space of fault-tolerant quantum systems design. Given a QEC program and a decoder, our verifier can quantify both the decoder accuracy and the decoder robustness to drift in physical error rate. Our approach has two key components: (i) a structured search over the space of possible errors; and (ii) a constrained polynomial optimization kernel. A thorough empirical evaluation of our approach suggests that it can outperform simulation, especially in low error rate regimes, and that it can be deployed to quantify decoder robustness over an interval of physical error rates.

</details>

### [5] [Computer-Orchestrated Design of Algorithms: From Join Specification to Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19434)
*Zeyuan Hu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CODA是一个计算机编排的测试框架，用于在物理协同设计过程中验证TreeTracker Join算法的逻辑到物理转换正确性，通过合成最小可复现示例来隔离细微的翻译缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库查询处理系统的理论保证与实际系统实现之间存在差距，逻辑规范到物理实现的转换正确性对实现理论最优性至关重要。现有测试框架难以处理算法特定输入（如连接树），且集成复杂算法工程开销大，宏观基准查询噪声太大无法隔离细微缺陷。

Method: 开发CODA测试框架，通过合成最小可复现示例来隔离逻辑到物理转换中的细微缺陷，如状态管理错误和连接树与bushy计划之间的映射冲突。该框架采用结构感知的测试生成流水线，专门针对依赖连接树的算法。

Result: CODA成功隔离了TreeTracker Join算法实现中的细微翻译缺陷，揭示了逻辑到物理转换是一个双向反馈循环：早期结构测试不仅强化了物理实现，还暴露了边界条件，直接改进了TTJ的形式化前提条件。

Conclusion: 逻辑到物理转换验证对实现理论最优算法的实际性能增益至关重要。CODA框架通过结构感知的测试生成，为依赖连接树的算法提供了有效的测试方法，其架构演化直接应对了翻译挑战，形成了稳健的测试流水线。

Abstract: Equipping query processing systems with provable theoretical guarantees has been a central focus at the intersection of database theory and systems in recent years. However, the divergence between theoretical abstractions and system assumptions creates a gap between an algorithm's high-level logical specification and its low-level physical implementation. Ensuring the correctness of this logical-to-physical translation is crucial for realizing theoretical optimality as practical performance gains. Existing database testing frameworks struggle to address this need because necessary algorithm-specific inputs such as join trees are absent from standard test case generation, and integrating complex algorithms into these frameworks imposes prohibitive engineering overhead. Fallback solutions, such as using macro-benchmark queries, are inherently too noisy for isolating intricate defects during this translation.
  In this experience paper, we present a retrospective analysis of $\mathsf{CODA}$, a computer-orchestrated testing framework utilized during the physical co-design of TreeTracker Join ($\mathsf{TTJ}$), a theoretically optimal yet practical join algorithm recently published in ACM TODS. By synthesizing minimal reproducible examples, $\mathsf{CODA}$ successfully isolates subtle translation defects, such as state mismanagement and mapping conflicts between join trees and bushy plans. We demonstrate that this logical-to-physical translation process is a bidirectional feedback loop: early structural testing not only hardened $\mathsf{TTJ}$'s physical implementation but also exposed a boundary condition that directly refined the formal precondition of $\mathsf{TTJ}$ itself. Finally, we detail how confronting these translation challenges drove the architectural evolution of $\mathsf{CODA}$ into a robust, structure-aware test generation pipeline for join-tree-dependent algorithms.

</details>


### [6] [Process Faster, Pay Less: Functional Isolation for Stream Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19445)
*Eleni Zapridou,Michael Koepf,Panagiotis Sioulas,Ioannis Mytilinis,Anastasia Ailamaki*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FunShare是一个流处理系统，通过动态分组查询来提高资源效率而不影响性能，减少冗余计算并适应数据流变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流处理引擎要么隔离查询资源导致高基础设施成本，要么共享工作但引入查询间干扰导致性能下降。需要一种既能提高资源效率又不损害性能的解决方案。

Method: FunShare采用自适应优化框架，动态分组基于性能特征的查询，策略性地放松查询间依赖关系，最小化冗余计算。系统监控执行指标，准确估计计算重叠，并根据底层数据流变化实时重新配置执行计划。

Result: 评估显示FunShare相比隔离执行最小化了资源消耗，同时为所有查询维持或提高了吞吐量。

Conclusion: FunShare通过动态查询分组和自适应优化，在流处理中实现了资源效率与性能的平衡，解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Concurrent workloads often extract insights from high-throughput, real-time data streams. Existing stream processing engines isolate each query's resources, ensuring robust performance but incurring high infrastructure costs. In contrast, sharing work reduces the amount of necessary resources but introduces inter-query interference, leading to performance degradation for some queries. We introduce FunShare, a stream-processing system that improves resource efficiency without compromising performance by dynamically grouping queries based on their performance characteristics. FunShare strategically relaxes query interdependencies and minimizes redundant computation while preserving individual query performance. It achieves this by using an adaptive optimization framework that monitors execution metrics, accurately estimates computation overlaps, and reconfigures execution plans on the fly in response to changes in the underlying data streams. Our evaluation demonstrates that FunShare minimizes resource consumption compared to isolated execution while maintaining or improving throughput for all queries.

</details>


### [7] [Low-Latency Stateful Stream Processing through Timely and Accurate Prefetching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19890)
*Eleni Zapridou,Anastasia Ailamaki*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出Keyed Prefetching和Timestamp-Aware Caching技术，通过提前提取状态访问密钥并主动预取状态，将状态I/O与计算重叠，降低流处理系统的延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键任务应用需要实时处理大量数据并保持低延迟。现代流处理引擎依赖键值存储管理状态，但状态访问会显著增加延迟。当前引擎将数据路径与状态I/O紧密耦合，当元组到达有状态算子时才触发状态访问，导致I/O处于关键路径上并阻塞CPU

Method: 1. Keyed Prefetching：从上游算子提前提取未来状态访问的密钥，在元组到达前主动将对应状态预取到内存中，实现数据路径与状态访问的解耦，使I/O与计算重叠。2. Timestamp-Aware Caching：缓存淘汰策略，联合管理先前访问和预取的条目，高效利用内存

Result: 这些技术共同降低了长期运行的实时查询的延迟，同时不牺牲吞吐量

Conclusion: 通过Keyed Prefetching和Timestamp-Aware Caching的组合，实现了状态访问与计算的解耦，有效隐藏了大状态访问的延迟，为关键任务流处理应用提供了低延迟解决方案

Abstract: Mission-critical applications often run "forever" and process large data volumes in real time while demanding low latency. To handle the large state of these applications, modern streaming engines rely on key-value stores and store state on local storage or remotely, but accessing such state inflates latency. As today's engines tightly couple the data path with state I/O, a tuple triggers state access only when it reaches a stateful operator, placing I/O on the critical path and stalling the CPU. However, the keys used to access the state are frequently known earlier in the query plan. Building on this insight, we propose Keyed Prefetching, which decouples the data path from state access by extracting future access keys at upstream operators and proactively staging the corresponding state in memory before tuples arrive. This overlaps I/O with ongoing computation and hides the latency of large-state accesses. We pair Keyed Prefetching with Timestamp-Aware Caching, a cache-eviction policy that jointly manages previously accessed and prefetched entries to use memory efficiently. Together, these techniques reduce latency for long-running, real-time queries without sacrificing throughput.

</details>

### [8] [Goedel-Code-Prover: Hierarchical Proof Search for Open State-of-the-Art Code Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19329)
*Zenan Li,Ziran Yang,Deyuan,He,Haoyu Zhao,Andrew Zhao,Shange Tang,Kaiyu Yang,Aarti Gupta,Zhendong Su,Chi Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出分层证明搜索框架用于Lean~4自动代码验证，通过分解复杂验证目标为简单子目标，训练8B参数统一策略模型，在三个基准测试中实现62.0%证明成功率，比最强基线提升2.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能生成看似合理的代码但正确性保证有限，形式化验证需要构建机器可检查的证明，这超出了当前自动化能力。需要开发能自动分解复杂验证目标的方法。

Method: 提出分层证明搜索框架，将复杂验证目标分解为结构更简单的子目标，然后进行策略级证明。核心是结合构造性证明和结构有效性的分解评分机制，该评分同时作为训练奖励和推理时排序标准。训练Goedel-Code-Prover-8B统一策略，通过监督初始化后混合强化学习，连续分解奖励驱动规划探索，监督回放稳定证明生成。

Result: 在包含427个任务的三个Lean代码验证基准测试中，8B参数模型达到62.0%证明成功率，比最强基线提升2.6倍，超越高达84倍大的神经证明器。推理时扩展性一致：成功率随搜索迭代和采样预算单调提升，训练模型比同规模前沿现成模型更高效。

Conclusion: 分层证明搜索框架通过分解复杂验证目标显著提升自动代码验证性能，统一策略模型在较小参数规模下实现优越性能，证明评分机制有效对齐训练和推理目标，为形式化验证自动化提供新途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate plausible code but offer limited guarantees of correctness. Formally verifying that implementations satisfy specifications requires constructing machine-checkable proofs, a task that remains beyond current automation. We propose a hierarchical proof search framework for automated code verification in Lean~4 that decomposes complex verification goals into structurally simpler subgoals before attempting tactic-level proving. Central to our approach is a principled decomposition score that combines constructive justification with structural effectiveness. Crucially, this score serves as both the training reward and the inference-time ranking criterion, ensuring strict alignment between optimization and deployment. We train Goedel-Code-Prover-8B, a single unified policy for both decomposition and completion, via supervised initialization followed by hybrid reinforcement learning, where a continuous decomposition reward drives planning exploration while supervised replay stabilizes proof generation. On three Lean-based code verification benchmarks comprising 427 tasks, our 8B-parameter model achieves a 62.0\% prove success rate, a 2.6$\times$ improvement over the strongest baseline, surpassing neural provers up to 84$\times$ larger. We further observe consistent inference-time scaling: success rates improve monotonically with search iterations and sampling budget, with our trained model achieving greater efficiency than frontier off-the-shelf models of comparable scale.

</details>


### [9] [GazePrinter: Visualizing Expert Gaze to Guide Novices in a New Codebase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19855)
*Peng Kuang,Emma Söderberg,April Yi Wang,Martin Höst*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GazePrinter：通过可视化专家凝视模式帮助新手程序员理解代码的工具，能引导新手更接近专家的代码阅读路径，减少时间和认知负荷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序理解是软件工程中的关键活动，对专业人员和初学者都具有挑战性。虽然凝视作为一种新兴模态已被用于研究程序员的视觉注意力和认知过程，但将基于凝视的辅助集成到开发环境中以支持程序员的研究还很有限。考虑到注意力与凝视之间的紧密联系，以及联合注意力在协作中的重要性，探索集体凝视的价值值得研究。

Method: 开发了GazePrinter工具，通过提供基于专家凝视信息的视觉线索来辅助新手进行程序理解。采用混合方法研究，对40名新手程序员进行了包含问卷调查、控制实验和访谈的研究，以评估GazePrinter在程序理解任务中的效果。

Result: 专家凝视的可视化对新手程序员的行为有显著影响：使用GazePrinter的新手在代码库中采取的路径更接近专家路径。同时发现使用GazePrinter的新手在时间和认知负荷方面有所减少的迹象。

Conclusion: 可视化专家凝视模式可以有效引导新手程序员在代码理解过程中采取更接近专家的路径，并可能减少理解时间和认知负担，为基于凝视的辅助工具在开发环境中的应用提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Program comprehension is an essential activity in software engineering. Not only does it often challenge professionals, but it can also hinder novices from advancing their programming skills. Gaze, an emerging modality in developer tools, has so far primarily been utilized to improve our understanding of programmers' visual attention and as a means to reason about programmers' cognitive processes. There has been limited exploration of integrating gaze-based assistance into development environments to support programmers, despite the tight links between attention and gaze. We also know that joint attention is important in collaboration, further suggesting that there is value in exploring collective gaze.
  In this paper, we investigate the effect of visualizing gaze patterns gathered from experts to novice programmers to assist them with program comprehension in a new codebase. To this end, we present GazePrinter, designed to provide gaze-orienting visual cues informed by experts to aid novices with program comprehension. We present the results of a mixed-methods study conducted with 40 novices to study the effects of using GazePrinter for program comprehension tasks. The study included a survey, a controlled experiment, and interviews. We found that visualization of expert gaze can have a significant effect on novice programmers' behavior in terms of which path they take through the code base; with GazePrinter, novices took a path closer to the path taken by experts. We also found indications of reduced time and cognitive load among novices using GazePrinter.

</details>


### [10] [Coordinating Stakeholders in the Consideration of Performance Indicators and Respective Interface Requirements for Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19492)
*Richard Schubert,Marvin Loba,Alexander Blödel,David Klüner,Alexandru Kampmann,Steven Peters*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个协调利益相关者考虑自动驾驶汽车性能指标和接口需求的流程，确保系统自感知实现的可追溯性和有效沟通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了让SAE Level 4自动驾驶汽车能够处理外部干扰和内部退化故障，需要实现自感知和自适应车辆。然而，缺乏系统化的利益相关者协调流程会导致架构决策不可追溯，利益相关者之间的有效沟通可能受损。

Method: 基于过程导向的方法，包含必要的要素、步骤和工件，通过清晰文档明确解决利益相关者沟通、可追溯性和知识转移问题。该方法基于在autotech.agil项目中的应用经验。

Result: 提出了一个系统化的协调流程，能够确保自动驾驶汽车性能指标和接口需求考虑过程中的利益相关者沟通、决策可追溯性和知识转移。

Conclusion: 该流程方法对于实现自动驾驶汽车的自感知和自适应能力至关重要，基于实际项目经验提供了经验教训、识别了差距，并提出了未来工作方向。

Abstract: This paper presents a process for coordinating stakeholders in their consideration of performance indicators and respective interface requirements for automated vehicles. These performance indicators are obtained and processed based on the system's self-perception and enable the realization of self-aware and self-adaptive vehicles. This is necessary to allow SAE Level 4 vehicles to handle external disturbances as well as internal degradations and failures at runtime. Without such a systematic process for stakeholder coordination, architectural decisions on realizing self-perception become untraceable and effective communication between stakeholders may be compromised. Our process-oriented approach includes necessary ingredients, steps, and artifacts that explicitly address stakeholder communication, traceability, and knowledge transfer through clear documentation. Our approach is based on the experience gained from applying the process in the autotech.agil project, from which we further present lessons learned, identified gaps, and steps for future work.

</details>


### [11] [DePro: Understanding the Role of LLMs in Debugging Competitive Programming Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19399)
*Nabiha Parvez,Tanvin Sarkar Pallab,Mia Mohammad Imran,Tarannum Shaila Zaman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DePro：一种基于测试用例驱动的LLM调试方法，通过暴力参考生成、压力测试和迭代式LLM引导优化，在竞争编程问题中有效识别和修复错误，相比人类程序员和零样本LLM调试显著减少调试尝试和时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调试在软件开发周期中占据重要部分，但大型语言模型在调试任务中的有效性尚未得到充分理解。竞争编程因其多样化问题领域和严格效率要求，为此类评估提供了丰富的基准。

Method: DePro采用测试用例驱动的调试方法，结合暴力参考生成（生成正确参考实现）、压力测试（通过随机输入比较参考实现与待调试代码的输出差异），以及迭代式LLM引导优化（基于失败测试用例指导LLM逐步修复代码错误）。

Result: 在Codeforces的13个错误用户提交上进行实验，DePro始终能生成正确解决方案，相比人类程序员和零样本LLM调试，调试尝试减少高达64%，每个问题的平均调试时间减少7.6分钟。

Conclusion: DePro展示了LLM在调试任务中的有效性，特别是在竞争编程场景下，通过系统化的测试用例驱动方法能够显著提高调试效率，减少开发者的调试负担。

Abstract: Debugging consumes a substantial portion of the software development lifecycle, yet the effectiveness of Large Language Models(LLMs) in this task is not well understood. Competitive programming offers a rich benchmark for such evaluation, given its diverse problem domains and strict efficiency requirements. We present an empirical study of LLM-based debugging on competitive programming problems and introduce DePro, a test-case driven approach that assists programmers by correcting existing code rather than generating new solutions. DePro combines brute-force reference generation, stress testing, and iterative LLM-guided refinement to identify and resolve errors efficiently.Experiments on 13 faulty user submissions from Codeforces demonstrate that DePro consistently produces correct solutions, reducing debugging attempts by up to 64% and debugging time by an average of 7.6 minutes per problem compared to human programmers and zero-shot LLM debugging.

</details>


### [12] [Revealing Domain-Spatiality Patterns for Configuration Tuning: Domain Knowledge Meets Fitness Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19897)
*Yulong Ye,Hongyuan Liang,Chao Jiang,Miqing Li,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Domland提出了一种结合适应度景观分析和领域知识的方法，用于解释配置调优中调优器成功或失败的原因，帮助理解调优器行为并指导调优器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置调优对质量保证至关重要，但由于可配置系统的黑盒特性，调优器有效性一直是个谜。先前方法主要采用静态领域分析（缺乏通用性）或动态数据分析（缺乏可解释性），需要一种能连接领域知识和调优难度的方法。

Method: 提出Domland方法，结合适应度景观分析（FLA）获取的空间信息和领域驱动分析，系统捕捉配置调优案例的隐藏特征，解释调优器成功或失败的原因。通过9个软件系统和93个工作负载的案例研究进行评估。

Result: 研究发现：(1)配置景观具有系统特异性，没有单一领域因素能一致塑造其结构；(2)控制主要功能流的核心选项比资源选项对景观崎岖度（调优难度）影响更大；(3)工作负载对景观结构的影响不均匀，取决于系统类型。

Conclusion: Domland通过结合适应度景观分析和领域知识，能够解释配置调优中调优器的行为，为调优器设计提供指导。配置景观具有系统特异性，核心选项对调优难度影响更大，工作负载影响因系统而异。

Abstract: Configuration tuning for better performance is crucial in quality assurance. Yet, there has long been a mystery on tuners' effectiveness, due to the black-box nature of configurable systems. Prior efforts predominantly adopt static domain analysis (e.g., static taint analysis), which often lacks generalizability, or dynamic data analysis (e.g., benchmarking performance analysis), limiting explainability. In this work, we embrace Fitness Landscape Analysis (FLA) as a bridge between domain knowledge and difficulty of the tuning. We propose Domland, a two-pronged methodology that synergizes the spatial information obtained from FLA and domain-driven analysis to systematically capture the hidden characteristics of configuration tuning cases, explaining how and why a tuner might succeed or fail. This helps to better interpret and contextualize the behavior of tuners and inform tuner design. To evaluate Domland, we conduct a case study of nine software systems and 93 workloads, from which we reveal several key findings: (1) configuration landscapes are inherently system-specific, with no single domain factor (e.g., system area, programming language, or resource intensity) consistently shaping their structure; (2) the core options (e.g., pic-struct of x264), which control the main functional flows, exert a stronger influence on landscape ruggedness (i.e. the difficulty of tuning) compared to resource options (e.g., cpu-independent of x264); (3) Workload effects on landscape structure are not uniformly tied to type or scale. Both contribute to landscape variations, but their impact is system-dependent.

</details>


### [13] [Teaching Practically Relevant Research Problem Formulation in Software Engineering with Lean Research Inception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19967)
*Anrafel Fernandes Pereira,Tatiane Ornelas,Allysson Allex Araujo,Marcos Kalinowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Lean Research Inception (LRI)方法应用于软件工程教育，帮助学生制定与实践对齐的研究问题，在巴西大学的案例研究中显示出积极效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究中，良好定义的研究问题对弥合学术界与工业界之间的鸿沟至关重要。Lean Research Inception (LRI)方法旨在支持这一活动，但需要在实际教育环境中验证其有效性。

Method: 在巴西一所大学进行案例研究，涉及60名学生和7名教师顾问，应用LRI方法来支持学生制定与实践对齐的研究问题。

Result: 学生报告在推理能力（60%）、清晰度和定义（61.7%）、情境化（60%）和沟通（50%）方面获得益处。教师顾问观察到更清晰和结构化的问题（57.1%），并给予高推荐率（85.7%）。

Conclusion: LRI是一种有前景的方法，能够有效支持软件工程教育中与实践对齐的研究问题制定。

Abstract: [Background] Well-formulated Software Engineering (SE) research problems are essential for bridging the gap between industry-academia. Lean Research Inception (LRI) aims to support this activity. [Goal] Apply LRI to support SE students in formulating practice-aligned research problems. [Method] We conducted a case study with 60 students and 7 faculty advisors of a Brazilian university. [Results] Students reported benefits in reasoning (60%), clarity and definition (61.7%), contextualization (60%), and communication (50%). Advisors also observed clearer and more structured problems (57.1%) with a high recommendation rate (85.7%). [Conclusion] LRI can be a promising approach to support practice-aligned research problem formulation in SE education.

</details>

### [14] [Diminishing Returns in Expanding Generative Models and Godel-Tarski-Lob Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19687)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个任务空间框架分析扩展生成系统，证明随着系统容量增加，可解决任务集的边际改进必然收敛于零，且存在逻辑推理的基本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成建模系统通过扩大模型容量、训练数据和计算资源不断改进，但扩展行为的理论极限仍不清楚。需要建立理论框架来分析扩展生成推理系统的能力增长极限。

Method: 1. 开发通用任务空间框架：将系统能力定义为在固定任务分布下可成功解决任务集的概率质量；2. 证明结构定理：在温和假设下，可解决任务的边际改进随系统容量增加收敛于零；3. 提出基于算法概率的复杂度加权假设类的预测理论细化；4. 分析逻辑推理任务，应用数学逻辑经典结果（Rosser不完全性、Tarski不可定义性、Löb定理）。

Result: 1. 证明扩展生成系统的边际能力改进必然渐近衰减；2. 提供预测设置中边际改进的定量界限；3. 展示在足够表达力的推理系统中，未解决的逻辑任务持续存在；4. 表明长期能力增长受任务覆盖的边际改进递减和内部推理的基本逻辑限制双重约束。

Conclusion: 扩展生成系统的渐近行为受数学约束：可解决任务的边际改进必然收敛于零，且存在逻辑推理的基本限制。这为理解生成系统扩展的极限提供了数学视角，表明单纯扩大规模存在根本性限制。

Abstract: Modern generative modelling systems are increasingly improved by expanding model capacity, training data, and computational resources. While empirical studies have documented such scaling behaviour across architectures including generative adversarial networks, variational autoencoders, transformer-based models, and diffusion models, the theoretical limits of capability growth in expanding generative systems remain poorly understood.
  In this paper we develop a general task-space framework for analysing expanding generative reasoning systems. Each system induces a subset of a global task space representing the tasks it can successfully solve, and system capability is measured by the probability mass of this solved-task set under a fixed task distribution. Within this framework we prove a structural result showing that, under mild assumptions, the marginal improvement in solved tasks must converge to zero as system capacity increases. Thus expanding generative systems may continue to gain capability, but the probability mass of newly solvable tasks necessarily diminishes asymptotically.
  We further provide a prediction-theoretic refinement based on complexity-weighted hypothesis classes inspired by algorithmic probability, yielding quantitative bounds on marginal improvement in prediction settings. Finally, we examine logical reasoning tasks and show that classical results from mathematical logic -- including Rosser incompleteness, Tarski's undefinability theorem, and Löb's theorem -- imply the persistence of unresolved logical tasks within sufficiently expressive reasoning systems.
  Together these results provide a mathematical perspective on the asymptotic behaviour of expanding generative systems, showing that long-run capability growth is constrained both by diminishing marginal improvements in task coverage and by fundamental logical limitations on internal reasoning.

</details>


### [15] [How Concise are Chains of co-Büchi Automata?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19806)
*Rüdiger Ehlers*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: COCOA（co-Büchi自动机链）作为ω-正则语言的新规范模型，本文分析了其简洁性：证明COCOA比确定性奇偶自动机更简洁，但布尔运算会导致指数级膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COCOA作为ω-正则语言的新规范表示模型，具有多项式时间最小化的优势，但对其与其他自动机模型的关系和布尔运算下的简洁性保持问题缺乏了解，需要系统分析其表达能力与代价。

Method: 通过理论分析证明三个主要结果：1）确定性COCOA比确定性奇偶自动机指数级简洁；2）二元布尔运算（析取、合取）会导致COCOA指数级膨胀；3）补运算也会导致指数级膨胀，因为需要重新分配识别颜色。

Result: 确定性COCOA比确定性奇偶自动机指数级简洁，但这种简洁性在布尔运算中无法保持：析取、合取和补运算都会导致COCOA表示规模的指数级膨胀，而奇偶自动机在相同运算中只需多项式增长。

Conclusion: COCOA虽然作为ω-正则语言的规范表示具有简洁性优势，但在实际应用中需要布尔运算时，这种简洁性会被抵消，因为布尔运算会导致指数级膨胀，而奇偶自动机在相同运算中表现更好。

Abstract: Chains of co-Büchi automata (COCOA) have recently been introduced as a new canonical model for representing arbitrary omega-regular languages. They can be minimized in polynomial time and are hence an attractive language representation for applications in which normally, deterministic omega-automata are used. While it is known how to build COCOA from deterministic parity automata, little is currently known about their relationship to automaton models introduced earlier than COCOA.
  In this paper, we analyze the conciseness of chains of co-Büchi automata. We provide three main results and give an overview of the implications of these results. First of all, we show that even in the case that all automata in the chain are deterministic, chains of co-Büchi automata can be exponentially more concise than deterministic parity automata. We then present two main results that together negatively answer the question if this conciseness is retained when performing Boolean operations (such as disjunction, conjunction, and complementation) over COCOA. For the binary operations, we show that there exist families of languages for which their application leads to an exponential growth of the sizes of the automata. The families have the property that when representing them using deterministic parity automata, taking the disjunction or conjunction of the family elements only requires a polynomial blow-up. We finally show that an exponential blow-up is also unavoidable when complementing a COCOA, as this operation can require redistributing with which colors words need to be recognized.

</details>


### [16] [Dynamically Reprogrammable Runtime Monitors for Bounded-time MTL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19851)
*Chirantan Hebballi,Akash Poptani,Amrutha Benny,Rajshekar Kalayappan,Sandeep Chandran,Ramchandra Phawade*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于标准单元而非FPGA的可重编程运行时验证监控器，支持在线、实时验证动态变化的属性，能够与待验证系统同芯片集成，实现高速监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于FPGA的运行时验证框架虽然支持现场操作期间属性动态变化，但难以跟上高性能处理器的速度。需要一种能够与待验证系统同芯片集成、支持实时监控动态属性的解决方案。

Method: 设计基于标准单元的可重编程监控器，包含实现五种基本操作的可编程单元和一组队列更新规则。通过组合这些可编程单元来忠实实现离散时间、有界MTL（度量时序逻辑）。

Result: 仿真表明监控器可通过I/O引脚在部署后重编程。支持最多16个原子命题的MTL公式的大型监控器仅占用0.55 mm²面积，工作频率达1.25 GHz。

Conclusion: 提出的基于标准单元的可重编程监控器解决了FPGA方案在性能上的不足，实现了与待验证系统同芯片集成的高速运行时验证，支持动态属性变化，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: A Runtime Verification (RV) framework that supports online, at-speed verification of properties that can change dynamically (during in-field operations) will benefit a large variety of applications. Several state-of-the-art RV frameworks propose to implement monitors on FPGAs. While this approach can support changes to the property being monitored during in-field operations, they struggle to keep pace with the system under verification which use high-performance processors. In this work, we propose a novel, reprogrammable monitor that is implemented using standard cells instead of FPGAs. This allows the monitor to be co-located with the system under verification (on the same die), and hence is amenable to at-speed monitoring of properties. Our proposed design consists of a programmable unit that implements five basic operations and a set of queue-update rules. We show that a composition of such programmable units faithfully implements discrete time, bounded MTL. We demonstrate through simulations that our proposed monitor can be reprogrammed (through its I/O pins) post deployment. A fairly large monitor which can support MTL formulae upto 16 atomic propositions occupies only 0.55 mm^2, while operating at a frequency of 1.25 GHz.

</details>

### [17] [Physical Layer Message Prediction for 5G Radio Access Network Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19760)
*Jonathan Ebert,Peter Rost*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 基于Transformer的5G NR协议逆向工程框架，利用5G标准上下文和语法预测物理层消息


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协议逆向工程是安全研究的前沿方法，需要开发能够逆向分析移动通信系统通信的框架，特别是针对3GPP发布的5G NR系统

Method: 利用5G标准的可用上下文和语法预测后续消息，采用Transformer模型，基于开源5G系统实现进行训练，模拟基站和多个用户设备

Result: 框架能够预测物理层的消息，实现了对5G通信协议的逆向工程能力

Conclusion: 提出的基于Transformer的框架成功实现了对5G NR系统通信的逆向工程，为移动通信安全研究提供了有效工具

Abstract: Protocol reverse engineering stands as the cutting-edge approach in security research. This paper presents a framework capable of reverse engineering the communications within a mobile communication system. Our focus is on systems released by the 3GPP, with an emphasis on 5G NR.
  Our approach leverages the available context and syntax of the 5G standard to predict subsequent messages. This approach relies on a Transformer model and is trained based on an open-source 5G system implementation, emulating a base station and several user equipments. The prediction targets messages at the physical layer.

</details>


### [18] [Implementing the L4S Architecture in the ns-3 Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20166)
*Maria Eduarda Veras,Eduardo Freitas,Assis T. de Oliveira Filho,Djamel Sadok,Judith Kelner*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 在ns-3中实现了L4S架构的端到端协议，包括从Linux内核移植TCP Prague和集成AccECN，解决了内核逻辑适配的工程挑战，验证了模型准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用对超低延迟的需求暴露了传统拥塞控制算法的局限性，特别是缓冲区膨胀问题。虽然Linux内核已有L4S实现，但研究社区缺乏ns-3中的完整高保真模型来进行可重复实验

Method: 在ns-3中实现L4S架构的端到端协议，重点包括：1) 从Linux内核(v6.12)移植TCP Prague；2) 集成AccECN信令；3) 解决Linux基于数据包的算术与ns-3基于字节的架构之间的适配挑战，特别是窗口管理和流量整形

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的模型能够准确复现真实测试场景中观察到的拥塞响应行为，验证了平台的准确性。该实现为复杂L4S性能评估提供了经过验证的工具集

Conclusion: 这项工作为研究社区提供了在受控环境中进行复杂L4S性能评估的验证工具集，解决了ns-3中缺乏完整L4S模型的问题，有助于可重复实验和进一步研究

Abstract: The demand for ultra-low latency in modern applications, such as cloud gaming and augmented reality, has exposed the limitations of traditional congestion control algorithms regarding bufferbloat. The Low Latency, Low Loss, and Scalable Throughput (L4S) architecture addresses this challenge by combining scalable congestion controls, such as TCP Prague, low-latency queue management with prioritization, and Accurate ECN (AccECN) feedback. Although Linux kernel implementations exist, the research community lacks a complete, high-fidelity model within the Network Simulator 3 (ns-3) for reproducible experiments. This paper presents an implementation of end-host protocols for the L4S architecture in ns-3, focusing on the porting of TCP Prague from the Linux kernel (v6.12) and the integration of AccECN signaling. Significant engineering challenges regarding the adaptation of kernel logic are detailed, particularly the reconciliation of Linux's packet-based arithmetic with ns-3's byte-based architecture for window management and pacing. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed model faithfully reproduces the congestion response behaviors observed in real-world testbed scenarios, validating the platform's accuracy. Consequently, this work provides the community with a validated toolset for complex L4S performance evaluations in controlled environments.

</details>
