### [41] [The Verifier Tax: Horizon Dependent Safety Success Tradeoffs in Tool Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19328)
*Tanmay Sah,Vishal Srivastava,Dolly Sah,Kayden Jordan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究运行时安全执行对多步骤工具使用LLM智能体端到端任务性能的影响，发现安全调解虽能拦截94%的不合规动作，但很少能实现严格的安全目标达成，存在持续的安全能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究运行时安全执行机制如何影响多步骤工具使用LLM智能体的端到端任务性能，特别关注安全调解对任务完成成功率的影响。

Method: 使用tau-bench在航空和零售领域进行实验，比较基线工具调用、规划集成(TRIAD)和政策调解(TRIAD-SAFETY)架构，使用GPT-OSS-20B和GLM-4-9B模型，分析模型依赖的交互视野(15-30轮)，将结果分解为总体成功率(SR)、安全成功率(SSR)和不安全成功率(USR)。

Result: 安全调解能拦截高达94%的不合规动作，但很少转化为严格的安全目标达成(大多数设置中SSR低于5%)。发现高不安全成功率主要由完整性泄露驱动，模型通过幻觉用户标识符绕过强制认证。被阻止动作后的恢复率持续较低，从GPT-OSS-20B在简单程序任务中的21%到复杂零售场景中的接近零。

Conclusion: 运行时安全执行对对话长度和计算成本施加了显著的验证器税，但不能保证安全完成，突显了对能够进行基于身份的验证和干预后推理的智能体的关键需求。

Abstract: We study how runtime enforcement against unsafe actions affects end-to-end task performance in multi-step tool using large language model (LLM) agents. Using tau-bench across Airline and Retail domains, we compare baseline Tool-Calling, planning-integrated (TRIAD), and policy-mediated (TRIAD-SAFETY) architectures with GPT-OSS-20B and GLM-4-9B. We identify model dependent interaction horizons (15 to 30 turns) and decompose outcomes into overall success rate (SR), safe success rate (SSR), and unsafe success rate (USR). Our results reveal a persistent Safety Capability Gap. While safety mediation can intercept up to 94 percent of non-compliant actions, it rarely translates into strictly safe goal attainment (SSR below 5 percent in most settings). We find that high unsafe success rates are primarily driven by Integrity Leaks, where models hallucinate user identifiers to bypass mandatory authentication. Recovery rates following blocked actions are consistently low, ranging from 21 percent for GPT-OSS-20B in simpler procedural tasks to near zero in complex Retail scenarios. These results demonstrate that runtime enforcement imposes a significant verifier tax on conversational length and compute cost without guaranteeing safe completion, highlighting the critical need for agents capable of grounded identity verification and post-intervention reasoning.

</details>


### [42] [Benchmarking Post-Quantum Cryptography on Resource-Constrained IoT Devices: ML-KEM and ML-DSA on ARM Cortex-M0+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19340)
*Rojin Chhetri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次在ARM Cortex-M0+上系统基准测试NIST后量子密码标准ML-KEM和ML-DSA，显示ML-KEM-512比ECDH P-256快17倍且能耗降低94%，ML-DSA签名延迟方差大，M0+仅比M4慢1.8-1.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备寿命长达10-20年，急需向后量子密码迁移，但目前缺乏在32位最受限处理器类别上对NIST最终标准的系统性基准测试。

Method: 在RP2040（Raspberry Pi Pico）的ARM Cortex-M0+处理器上，使用PQClean参考C实现，测量ML-KEM和ML-DSA所有三个安全级别的密钥生成、封装/签名、解封装/验证性能。

Result: ML-KEM-512完整密钥交换耗时36.3ms，能耗2.87mJ，比同硬件上的ECDH P-256快17倍且能耗降低94%；ML-DSA签名因拒绝采样导致高延迟方差（变异系数61-71%）；M0+仅比已发布的M4结果慢1.8-1.9倍。

Conclusion: 后量子密码在受限物联网设备上可行，ML-KEM性能优异，ML-DSA签名延迟方差需关注，M0+处理器表现超出预期，开源基准套件促进可重复性研究。

Abstract: The migration to post-quantum cryptography is urgent for Internet of Things devices with 10-20 year lifespans, yet no systematic benchmarks exist for the finalised NIST standards on the most constrained 32-bit processor class. This paper presents the first isolated algorithm-level benchmarks of ML-KEM (FIPS 203) and ML-DSA (FIPS 204) on ARM Cortex-M0+, measured on the RP2040 (Raspberry Pi Pico) at 133 MHz with 264 KB SRAM. Using PQClean reference C implementations, we measure all three security levels of ML-KEM (512/768/1024) and ML-DSA (44/65/87) across key generation, encapsulation/signing, and decapsulation/verification. ML-KEM-512 completes a full key exchange in 36.3 ms consuming 2.87 mJ--17x faster and 94% less energy than ECDH P-256 on the same hardware. ML-DSA signing exhibits high latency variance due to rejection sampling (coefficient of variation 61-71%, 99th-percentile up to 1,115 ms for ML-DSA-87). The M0+ incurs only a 1.8-1.9x slowdown relative to published Cortex-M4 results, despite lacking 64-bit multiply, DSP, and SIMD instructions. All code, data, and scripts are released as an open-source benchmark suite for reproducibility.

</details>


### [43] [A Framework for Formalizing LLM Agent Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19469)
*Vincent Siu,Jingxuan He,Kyle Montgomery,Zhun Wang,Neil Gong,Chenguang Wang,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个基于上下文安全视角的LLM智能体安全框架，将现有攻击和防御重新形式化为四个安全属性的违反或增强机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体安全定义缺乏对上下文依赖性的考虑，导致防御措施面临效用-安全权衡困境：统一应用防御会导致显著效用损失，而不充分或不适当应用则会造成安全漏洞

Method: 提出一个系统化框架，包含四个捕捉LLM智能体上下文安全的安全属性：任务对齐、行动对齐、来源授权和数据隔离；引入一组预言机函数来验证这些安全属性是否被违反

Result: 将现有攻击（如间接提示注入、直接提示注入、越狱、任务漂移和内存污染）重新形式化为一个或多个安全属性的违反，为这些攻击提供精确的上下文定义；将防御重新形式化为增强预言机函数或执行安全属性检查的机制

Conclusion: 该框架为LLM智能体安全提供了系统化的上下文视角，能够更精确地定义攻击和防御，并开启了多个重要的未来研究方向

Abstract: Security in LLM agents is inherently contextual. For example, the same action taken by an agent may represent legitimate behavior or a security violation depending on whose instruction led to the action, what objective is being pursued, and whether the action serves that objective. However, existing definitions of security attacks against LLM agents often fail to capture this contextual nature. As a result, defenses face a fundamental utility-security tradeoff: applying defenses uniformly across all contexts can lead to significant utility loss, while applying defenses in insufficient or inappropriate contexts can result in security vulnerabilities. In this work, we present a framework that systematizes existing attacks and defenses from the perspective of contextual security. To this end, we propose four security properties that capture contextual security for LLM agents: task alignment (pursuing authorized objectives), action alignment (individual actions serving those objectives), source authorization (executing commands from authenticated sources), and data isolation (ensuring information flows respect privilege boundaries). We further introduce a set of oracle functions that enable verification of whether these security properties are violated as an agent executes a user task. Using this framework, we reformalize existing attacks, such as indirect prompt injection, direct prompt injection, jailbreak, task drift, and memory poisoning, as violations of one or more security properties, thereby providing precise and contextual definitions of these attacks. Similarly, we reformalize defenses as mechanisms that strengthen oracle functions or perform security property checks. Finally, we discuss several important future research directions enabled by our framework.

</details>


### [44] [Cellular Automata based Resource Efficient Maximally Equidistributed Pseudo-Random Number Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19656)
*Bhuvaneswari A,Kamalika Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了现有线性细胞自动机伪随机数生成器在均匀分布特性上的缺陷，提出了一组轻量级组合CA-PRNGs，证明其具有最大周期和最大均匀分布特性，并通过了大多数经验测试，性能与梅森旋转算法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于线性细胞自动机的伪随机数生成器在均匀分布特性上表现不佳，需要设计具有更好统计特性的轻量级PRNGs。

Method: 使用线性最大长度细胞自动机（度数为31-128，接近计算机字长），构建时间间隔为2-10的轻量级组合CA-PRNGs。

Result: 提出的组合CA-PRNGs实现了最大周期，满足最大均匀分布特性，通过了几乎所有经验测试，速度和性能与梅森旋转算法相当。

Conclusion: 提出的组合最大长度CA-PRNGs在均匀分布特性上优于现有线性CA-PRNGs，具有优异的统计特性和实用性能。

Abstract: An equidistribution is a theoretical quality criteria that measures the uniformity of a linear pseudo-random number generator (PRNG). In this work, we first show that all existing linear cellular automaton (CA) based pseudo-random number generators (PRNGs) are weak in the equidistribution characteristic. Then we propose a list of light-weight combined CA-based PRNGs with time spacing ($2 \leq s \leq 10$) using linear maximal length cellular automata of degree $31 \leq k \leq 128$ (close to computer word size). We show that these PRNGs achieve maximal period as well as satisfy the maximal equidistribution property. Finally, we show that these combined maximal length CA-based PRNGs pass almost all the empirical testbeds, with speed and performance comparable to the Mersenne Twister.

</details>


### [45] [ProHunter: A Comprehensive APT Hunting System Based on Whole-System Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19658)
*Xuebo Qiu,Mingqi Lv,Yimei Zhang,Tiantian Zhu,Tieming Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ProHunter：一种高效准确的基于溯源图的APT狩猎系统，通过语义抽象和分层编码减少开销，使用启发式采样算法提取攻击模式，并采用自适应图表示方法弥合CTI与溯源图之间的语义鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于溯源图的威胁狩猎面临三大挑战：1）溯源图规模庞大导致内存和时间开销巨大；2）APT活动与良性操作交织导致攻击模式分割不精确；3）CTI查询图与溯源图之间存在语义鸿沟导致攻击表示对齐困难。

Method: 1）采用语义抽象和位级分层编码策略构建紧凑数据结构存储长期溯源图；2）设计启发式威胁图采样算法从溯源图中精确提取攻击模式；3）提出自适应图表示和特征增强方法，在局部和全局层面提取一致的攻击语义。

Result: 在DARPA TC E3、E5和OpTC数据集上的真实APT活动评估表明，ProHunter在效率和准确性方面均优于现有最先进的威胁狩猎系统。

Conclusion: ProHunter通过平台无关的设计，有效解决了基于溯源的APT狩猎中的效率、精确性和语义对齐问题，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) remain difficult to detect due to their stealthy nature and long-term persistence. To tackle this challenge, provenance-based threat hunting has gained traction as a proactive defense mechanism. This technique models audit logs as a whole-system provenance graph and searches for subgraphs that match APT patterns recorded in Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) reports. However, several limitations persist: 1) significant memory and time overhead due to the extremely large provenance graphs; 2) imprecise segmentation of APT activities from provenance graphs due to their intricate entanglement with benign operations; and 3) poor alignment of attack representations between CTI-derived query graphs and provenance graphs due to their substantial semantic gaps.
  To address these limitations, this paper presents ProHunter, an efficient and accurate provenance-based APT hunting system with a platform-independent design. To minimize system overhead, ProHunter creates a compact data structure that efficiently stores long-term provenance graphs using semantic abstraction and bit-level hierarchical encoding strategies. To segment APT behaviors, a heuristic-driven threat graph sampling algorithm is designed, which can extract precise attack patterns from provenance graphs. Furthermore, to bridge the semantic gaps between CTI-derived graphs and provenance graphs, ProHunter proposes adaptive graph representation and feature enhancement methods, enabling the extraction of consistent attack semantics at both localized and globalized levels.Extensive evaluations on real-world APT campaigns from DARPA TC E3, E5 and OpTC datasets demonstrate that ProHunter outperforms state-of-the-art threat hunting systems in terms of efficiency and accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/xueboQiu/ProHunter.

</details>


### [46] [From Precise to Random: A Systematic Differential Fault Analysis of the Lightweight Block Cipher Lilliput](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19781)
*Peipei Xie,Siwei Chen,Zejun Xiang,Shasha Zhang,Xiangyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次对Lilliput密码进行系统性的差分故障分析，在三种逐步放宽攻击假设的故障模型下评估其故障抵抗能力，发现存在显著安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lilliput密码虽然对理论攻击（差分、线性、回旋、积分攻击）具有足够安全性，但缺乏对物理攻击（特别是故障攻击）的抵抗性分析。需要评估其在真实场景中的故障恢复能力。

Method: 采用三种逐步放宽攻击假设的nibble导向故障模型：模型I（多轮固定位置）、模型II（单轮固定位置）、模型III（单轮随机位置）。利用Lilliput的扩散特性和DDT约束进行差分故障分析。

Result: 模型I：在特定轮次精确注入故障，仅需8个故障即可以98%成功率恢复主密钥。模型II：单轮固定位置故障，8个故障仍能达到99%成功率。模型III：第27轮右8个分支随机位置故障，33个故障成功率超99%，36个故障超99.5%。

Conclusion: Lilliput在不同攻击者能力的真实场景下存在显著的故障攻击漏洞，为其安全实现提供了重要见解。需要加强物理安全防护措施。

Abstract: At SAC 2013, Berger et al. first proposed the Extended Generalized Feistel Networks (EGFN) structure for the design of block ciphers with efficient diffusion. Later, based on the Type-2 EGFN, they instantiated a new lightweight block cipher named Lilliput (published in IEEE Transactions on Computers, Vol. 65, Issue 7, 2016). According to published cryptanalysis results, Lilliput is sufficiently secure against theoretical attacks such as differential, linear, boomerang, and integral attacks, which rely on the statistical properties of plaintext and ciphertext. However, there is a lack of analysis regarding its resistance to physical attacks in real-world scenarios, such as fault attacks. In this paper, we present the first systematic differential fault analysis (DFA) of Lilliput under three nibble-oriented fault models with progressively relaxed adversarial assumptions to comprehensively assess its fault resilience. In Model I (multi-round fixed-location), precise fault injections at specific rounds recover the master key with a 98% success rate using only 8 faults. Model II (single-round fixed-location) relaxes the multi-round requirement, demonstrating that 8 faults confined to a single round are still sufficient to achieve a 99% success rate by exploiting Lilliput's diffusion properties and DDT-based constraints. Model III (single-round random-location) further weakens the assumption by allowing faults to occur randomly among the eight rightmost branches of round 27. By uniquely identifying the fault location from ciphertext differences with high probability, the attack remains highly feasible, achieving over 99% success with 33 faults and exceeding 99.5% with 36 faults. Our findings reveal a significant vulnerability of Lilliput to practical fault attacks across different adversary capabilities in real-world scenarios, providing crucial insights for its secure implementation.

</details>


### [47] [Kumo: A Security-Focused Serverless Cloud Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19787)
*Wei Shao,Khaled Khasawneh,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun,Chongzhou Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Kumo是一个专注于安全的无服务器平台模拟器，用于分析调度和资源共享决策带来的安全风险，包括共置攻击和拒绝服务攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器计算虽然简化了基础设施管理，但隐藏了系统级行为，这些行为可能引入安全风险。现有研究已表明无服务器平台易受共享执行环境攻击，但在生产平台上分析这些风险存在困难：可观测性有限、成本高、缺乏实验控制，而现有模拟器主要关注性能和成本而非安全。

Method: 开发Kumo安全模拟器，采用离散事件框架建模调用到达、调度器放置、容器重用、资源争用和排队。将攻击者和受害者作为一等实体明确表示，提供共置概率、首次共置时间、调用丢弃率和尾部延迟等指标。

Result: 通过两个案例研究表明：1) 调度器选择是共置攻击的一阶因素，在相同工作负载下可导致数量级差异；2) 拒绝服务行为主要由系统级因素（服务时间、排队策略、集群容量）控制，一旦争用占主导。

Conclusion: 需要区分调度器驱动的隔离风险与更广泛的资源耗尽漏洞，Kumo为系统化、安全感知的无服务器平台探索提供了灵活基础。

Abstract: Serverless computing abstracts infrastructure management but also obscures system-level behaviors that can introduce security risks. Prior work has shown that serverless platforms are vulnerable to attacks exploiting shared execution environments, including attacker--victim co-location and denial-of-service through resource contention, yet analyzing these risks on production platforms is difficult due to limited observability, high cost, and lack of experimental control, while existing simulators primarily focus on performance and cost rather than security. We present Kumo, a security-focused simulator for serverless platforms that enables controlled, reproducible analysis of security risks arising from scheduling and resource sharing decisions. Kumo models invocation arrivals, scheduler placement, container reuse, resource contention, and queuing within a discrete-event framework, explicitly representing attackers and victims as first-class entities and providing metrics such as co-location probability, time to first co-location, invocation drop rate, and tail latency. Through two case studies, we show that scheduler choice is a first-order factor for co-location attacks, inducing orders-of-magnitude differences under identical workloads, while Denial-of-Service behavior is largely governed by system-level factors such as service time, queuing policy, and cluster capacity once contention dominates. These results highlight the need to distinguish scheduler-driven isolation risks from broader resource exhaustion vulnerabilities and position Kumo as a flexible foundation for systematic, security-aware exploration of serverless platforms.

</details>


### [48] [Case Study: Horizontal Side-Channel Analysis Attack against Elliptic Curve Scalar Multiplication Accelerator under Laser Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19811)
*Dmytro Petryk,Ievgen Kabin,Peter Langendoerfer,Zoya Dyka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合侧信道分析和激光照射攻击的新方法（SCuLI攻击），针对椭圆曲线标量乘法加速器，通过测量和分析激光照射下的静态电流来增强攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密设备需要抵抗物理攻击，特别是侧信道分析（SCA）和故障注入（FI）攻击。目前针对公钥密码系统的水平攻击方法仍有改进空间，尤其是在先进制程技术下，激光照射对静态功耗的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 采用Teledyne LeCroy差分探头，对椭圆曲线标量乘法加速器实施结合侧信道分析和激光照射的攻击。通过激光照射增加芯片功耗（特别是静态功耗），并测量分析激光照射下的静态电流变化。

Result: 实验显示激光照射确实增加了芯片功耗，特别是静态功耗，但对水平功耗分析攻击的成功率改变不显著。作者推测使用更高功率激光并专注于测量分析静态电流可显著提高攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 基于激光照射下静态功耗的水平攻击（SCuLI攻击）是新颖且具有潜力的攻击方法，尤其对采用先进制程技术的加密芯片构成威胁。如果此类攻击可行，未来需要提出相应的防护措施。

Abstract: Devices employing cryptographic approaches have to be resistant to physical attacks. Side-Channel Analysis (SCA) and Fault Injection (FI) attacks are frequently used to reveal cryptographic keys. In this paper, we present a combined SCA and laser illumination attack against an Elliptic Curve Scalar Multiplication accelerator using a differential probe from Teledyne LeCroy. Our experiments show that laser illumination increases the power consumption of the chip, especially its static power consumption but the success of the horizontal power analysis attacks was changed insignificantly. We assume that using a laser with a high laser beam power and concentrating on measuring and analysing only static current can improve the attack success significantly. The horizontal attacks against public key cryptosystems exploiting the Static Consumption under Laser Illumination (SCuLI attacks) are novel and their potential is not investigated yet. These attacks can be especially dangerous against cryptographic chips manufactured in scaled technologies. If such attacks are feasible, appropriate countermeasures have to be proposed in the future.

</details>


### [49] [A Theory of Composable Lingos for Protocol Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19908)
*Víctor García,Santaigo Escobar,Catherine Meadows,Jose Meseguer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将形式化模式应用于协议方言，通过形式化模式增强方言中的安全组件（lingo）和方言本身，为分布式系统提供轻量级安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化模式为分布式系统问题提供具有形式化保证的通用解决方案。本文旨在将形式化模式应用于协议方言，以增强协议的安全性，特别是针对可能导致更严重攻击的简单攻击。方言通过其核心安全组件（lingo）使攻击者无法"说"该语言，从而提供轻量级安全防护。

Method: 提出多种形式化模式：1）用于lingo的形式化模式，通过转换单个lingo或将多个lingo组合成更强的lingo来增强安全性；2）用于方言的形式化模式，该模式在选定的lingo和协议上具有通用性，可生成方言。这些模式是通用的系统转换，能将需要解决方案的系统类转换为增强版本。

Result: 本文展示了形式化模式在协议方言中的应用，提出了增强lingo和生成方言的具体形式化模式。这些模式提供了形式化保证，使lingo成为攻击者难以应对的"移动目标"，并通过通用模式生成具有安全增强的方言。

Conclusion: 形式化模式可有效应用于协议方言的安全增强。通过lingo形式化模式可强化安全组件，通过方言形式化模式可生成具有轻量级安全防护的协议变体。这种方法为分布式系统提供了具有形式化保证的安全解决方案。

Abstract: Formal patterns are formally specified solutions to frequently occurring distributed system problems that are generic, executable, and come with strong qualitative and/or quantitative formal guarantees. A formal pattern is a generic system transformation which transforms a usually infinite class of systems in need of the pattern's solution into enhanced versions of such systems that solve the problem in question. In this paper we demonstrate the application of formal patterns to protocol dialects. Dialects are methods for hardening protocols so as to endow them with light-weight security, especially against easy attacks that can lead to more serious ones. A lingo is a dialect's key security component, because attackers are unable to ''speak'' the lingo. A lingo's ''talk'' changes all the time, becoming a moving target for attackers. In this paper we present several formal patterns for both lingos and dialects. Lingo formal patterns can make lingos stronger by both transforming them and by composing several lingos into a stronger lingo. Dialects themselves can be obtained by the application of a single dialect formal pattern, generic on both the chosen lingo and the chosen protocol.

</details>


### [50] [TAPAS: Efficient Two-Server Asymmetric Private Aggregation Beyond Prio(+)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19949)
*Harish Karthikeyan,Antigoni Polychroniadou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TAPAS是一个非对称双服务器隐私保护聚合方案，通过让一个服务器承担O(L)计算负担、另一个服务器保持轻量级操作，解决了现有对称协议在通信和计算成本方面随输入维度L线性增长的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双服务器协议（如Prio及其后继者）虽然能验证输入并防止单方学习用户数据，但对两个服务器施加对称成本，且通信开销随客户端输入维度L线性增长。现代学习任务通常涉及数千万到数亿维度的模型参数，这种线性扩展性成为瓶颈。

Method: 采用非对称设计：一个服务器承担O(L)的聚合和验证工作，另一个作为轻量级协调者，计算与L无关。基于标准格假设（LWE、SIS）构建后量子安全方案，引入新的高效格基零知识证明，首次在双服务器设置中实现可识别中止的隐私和正确性。

Result: TAPAS实现了服务器端通信与L无关，无需可信设置或预处理，提供后量子安全性，并具备更强的鲁棒性，包括可识别中止和针对服务器的完全恶意安全性。非对称设计降低了总成本，使次要服务器可在商用硬件上运行，并加强了服务器的非共谋假设。

Conclusion: TAPAS通过创新的非对称架构和新的格基零知识证明，解决了现有隐私保护聚合方案在可扩展性和成本方面的关键限制，为大规模分布式学习任务提供了更实用、高效且安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving aggregation is a cornerstone for AI systems that learn from distributed data without exposing individual records, especially in federated learning and telemetry. Existing two-server protocols (e.g., Prio and successors) set a practical baseline by validating inputs while preventing any single party from learning users' values, but they impose symmetric costs on both servers and communication that scales with the per-client input dimension $L$. Modern learning tasks routinely involve dimensionalities $L$ in the tens to hundreds of millions of model parameters.
  We present TAPAS, a two-server asymmetric private aggregation scheme that addresses these limitations along four dimensions: (i) no trusted setup or preprocessing, (ii) server-side communication that is independent of $L$ (iii) post-quantum security based solely on standard lattice assumptions (LWE, SIS), and (iv) stronger robustness with identifiable abort and full malicious security for the servers. A key design choice is intentional asymmetry: one server bears the $O(L)$ aggregation and verification work, while the other operates as a lightweight facilitator with computation independent of $L$. This reduces total cost, enables the secondary server to run on commodity hardware, and strengthens the non-collusion assumption of the servers. One of our main contributions is a suite of new and efficient lattice-based zero-knowledge proofs; to our knowledge, we are the first to establish privacy and correctness with identifiable abort in the two-server setting.

</details>


### [51] [Sharing The Secret: Distributed Privacy-Preserving Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20107)
*Mahyar Karimi,K. S. Thejaswini,Roderick Bloem,Thomas A. Henzinger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式隐私保护运行时监控协议，通过将监控器分布在多个诚实方之间，使用高效秘密共享替代重加密，显著提升可扩展性，支持带秘密状态的连续监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运行时验证采用单点监控器，隐私保护需要昂贵加密原语，导致实时应用不可行。现有秘密共享方法仅限于一次性执行，无法维护内部状态。

Method: 将监控器分布在多个参与方（至少一方诚实），使用高效秘密共享方案替代计算密集型密码学。设计支持连续监控的协议，能够维护演化中的秘密内部状态（对系统和监控实体保密）。基于MP-SPDZ框架实现。

Result: 实验表明，在给定架构假设下，该协议比现有方案显著更具可扩展性，大幅降低开销同时保持强隐私保证。

Conclusion: 通过分布式监控架构和定制秘密共享协议，解决了隐私保护运行时验证的可扩展性挑战，使实时应用成为可能。

Abstract: In traditional runtime verification, a system is typically observed by a monolithic monitor. Enforcing privacy in such settings is computationally expensive, as it necessitates heavy cryptographic primitives. Therefore, privacy-preserving monitoring remains impractical for real-time applications. In this work, we address this scalability challenge by distributing the monitor across multiple parties -- at least one of which is honest. This architecture enables the use of efficient secret-sharing schemes instead of computationally intensive cryptography, dramatically reducing over-head while maintaining strong privacy guarantees. While existing secret-sharing approaches are typically limited to one-shot executions which do not maintain an internal state, we introduce a protocol tailored for continuous monitoring that supports repeated evaluations over an evolving internal state (kept secret from the system and the monitoring entities). We implement our approach using the MP-SPDZ framework. Our experiments demonstrate that, under these architectural assumptions, our protocol is significantly more scalable than existing alternatives.

</details>


### [52] [Evolving Jailbreaks: Automated Multi-Objective Long-Tail Attacks on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20122)
*Wenjing Hong,Zhonghua Rong,Li Wang,Feng Chang,Jian Zhu,Ke Tang,Zexuan Zhu,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvoJail：基于多目标进化搜索的自动化框架，用于发现针对大语言模型的长尾分布攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过免费Web应用广泛部署，暴露于用户生成的长尾分布输入（如低资源语言、加密私有数据），增加了越狱攻击风险。现有方法依赖手工规则，难以系统评估这些安全隐私漏洞。

Method: 提出EvoJail框架，将长尾攻击提示生成建模为多目标优化问题，最大化攻击效果同时最小化输出困惑度。引入语义-算法解决方案表示，捕获高层语义意图和低层加密解密逻辑的结构变换，并集成LLM辅助算子到多目标进化框架中。

Result: 大量实验表明，EvoJail能一致地发现多样且有效的长尾越狱策略，在个体和集成层面均与现有方法竞争。

Conclusion: EvoJail为系统评估大语言模型在长尾分布下的安全隐私漏洞提供了自动化框架，通过进化搜索有效发现越狱攻击策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely deployed, especially through free Web-based applications that expose them to diverse user-generated inputs, including those from long-tail distributions such as low-resource languages and encrypted private data. This open-ended exposure increases the risk of jailbreak attacks that undermine model safety alignment. While recent studies have shown that leveraging long-tail distributions can facilitate such jailbreaks, existing approaches largely rely on handcrafted rules, limiting the systematic evaluation of these security and privacy vulnerabilities. In this work, we present EvoJail, an automated framework for discovering long-tail distribution attacks via multi-objective evolutionary search. EvoJail formulates long-tail attack prompt generation as a multi-objective optimization problem that jointly maximizes attack effectiveness and minimizes output perplexity, and introduces a semantic-algorithmic solution representation to capture both high-level semantic intent and low-level structural transformations of encryption-decryption logic. Building upon this representation, EvoJail integrates LLM-assisted operators into a multi-objective evolutionary framework, enabling adaptive and semantically informed mutation and crossover for efficiently exploring a highly structured and open-ended search space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvoJail consistently discovers diverse and effective long-tail jailbreak strategies, achieving competitive performance with existing methods in both individual and ensemble level.

</details>


### [53] [HQC Post-Quantum Cryptography Decryption with Generalized Minimum-Distance Reed-Solomon Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20156)
*Jiaxuan Cai,Xinmiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对后量子密码标准HQC，提出使用广义最小距离(GMD)软判决解码器替代现有的擦除解码器，显著提升解码性能，可将RS码字长度从46减少到36，并设计高效硬件架构，使HQC-128解密延迟和面积分别降低20%和15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HQC后量子密码标准中使用的级联RM-RS码，现有软判决解码器仅限于擦除解码器，编码增益有限。需要探索其他硬件友好的软判决RS解码器来更好地利用HQC中的软信息，从而缩短码字和密钥长度。

Method: 分析多种硬件友好的软判决RS解码器，发现广义最小距离(GMD)解码器能更好地利用HQC中的软信息。扩展Agrawal-Vardy界到HQC场景，提出针对HQC中短码、低码率RS码优化的高效GMD解码器硬件架构。

Result: 发现GMD解码器可将HQC-128中的RS码字长度从46减少到36。提出的GMD解码器硬件架构使HQC-128解密延迟降低20%，面积减少15%，相比硬判决解码器有显著改进。

Conclusion: GMD软判决解码器比现有的擦除解码器能更有效地利用HQC中的软信息，显著提升解码性能并减少硬件开销，为后量子密码HQC提供了更高效的硬件实现方案。

Abstract: Hamming Quasi-Cyclic (HQC) was chosen for the latest post-quantum cryptography standardization. A concatenated Reed-Muller (RM) and Reed-Solomon (RS) code is decoded during the HQC decryption. Soft-decision RS decoders achieve better error-correcting performance than hard-decision decoders and accordingly shorten the required codeword and key lengths. However, the only soft-decision decoder for HQC in prior works is an erasure-only decoder, which has limited coding gain. This paper analyzes other hardware-friendly soft-decision RS decoders and discovers that the generalized minimum-distance (GMD) decoder can better utilize the soft information available in HQC. Extending the Agrawal-Vardy bound for the scenario of HQC, it was found that the RS codeword length for HQC-128 can be reduced from 46 to 36. This paper also proposes efficient GMD decoder hardware architectures optimized for the short and low-rate RS codes used in HQC. The HQC-128 decryption utilizing the proposed GMD decoder achieves 20% and 15% reductions on the latency and area, respectively, compared to the decryption with hard-decision decoders.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [54] [It Depends: Re_Authoring Play Through Clinical Reasoning in Wearable AR Rehab Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19309)
*Binyan Xu,Wei Wu,Soonhyeon Kweon,Casper Harteveld,Leanne Chukoskie*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 系统综述132个AR康复应用并结合14名物理治疗师测试，提出治疗师重新设计AR游戏的三种方式及临床推理框架，为个性化康复游戏设计提供原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强现实游戏在康复领域潜力巨大，但多数研究局限于实验室，临床转化有限。轻量化眼镜式AR设备的出现为将康复游戏融入临床实践和日常生活提供了新机遇。

Method: 对132个AR康复应用进行系统综述，并与14名持证物理治疗师进行游戏测试，分析治疗师如何重新设计AR游戏以适应临床需求。

Result: 发现治疗师通过三种方式重新设计AR游戏：共同创作游戏（调整动作、进度和难度）、情境化游戏（适应不同专科、条件和环境）、双重游戏（兼顾身体康复和心理支持）。将治疗师常说的"视情况而定"重构为生成性设计原则。

Conclusion: 研究提出了基于临床推理的框架和设计原则，为创建个性化、情境化的康复游戏提供指导，这些游戏能与治疗师的日常工作流程相结合，促进实验室到临床的转化。

Abstract: Augmented reality games hold promise for rehabilitation, yet most remain confined to laboratory studies with limited clinical uptake. Recent advances in spatial computing, especially lightweight, glasses_form_factor AR, create a timely opportunity to embed rehabilitative play into clinical practice and daily contexts. To investigate this potential, we systematically reviewed 132 applications and conducted playtesting with 14 licensed physical therapists. Our analysis revealed three ways therapists re_authored AR games: co_authored play (reshaping movements, progressions, and difficulty), situated play (adapting across specialties, conditions, and contexts), and dual play (mediating both physical recovery and psychological support). We reframe therapists' frequent phrase_It depends_as a generative design principle. This study contributes a clinical reasoning_based framework and design principles and guidelines for creating personalized, situated forms of play that align with therapists' everyday workflows and inform future lab_to_clinic translation.

</details>


### [55] [Strategies for Designing Responsibly within a Capitalist Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19400)
*Shixian Xie,Motahhare Eslami,John Zimmerman*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该立场论文主张负责任AI研究需要与资本主义企业创造价值的需求相协调，提出超越"伦理vs商业"二分法，通过构思策略生成既符合伦理又满足商业目标的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管负责任AI研究取得显著进展，但行业采用仍然有限，许多HCI贡献在实践中未得到充分利用。当前研究往往未能考虑资本主义企业创造价值的基本需求，导致研究成果难以落地。

Method: 提出将"伦理vs商业"的二分法转变为"伦理与商业"的协同框架，建议采用构思作为实用设计策略，生成既符合伦理偏好又满足商业目标的替代方案。

Result: 通过将伦理与企业现实对齐，扩展了能够实际构建的负责任设计空间，使负责任AI研究能够更好地适应资本主义环境下的实际需求。

Conclusion: 为实现现实世界影响，负责任AI研究必须探索如何在资本主义框架内进行负责任设计，通过伦理与商业的协同来推动实际应用和采纳。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in responsible AI research, industry adoption remains limited, leaving many HCI contributions underutilized in practice. This position paper argues that current research often fails to account for the fundamental need for capitalist enterprises to create value. To achieve immediate real-world impact, responsible AI research must explore how to design responsibly within capitalism. We call for a move beyond the dichotomy of "ethics vs. business" toward a more productive framing of "ethics and business." We propose ideation as a practical design strategy for generating ethically preferable alternatives that also meet business objectives. By aligning ethics with enterprise realities, we expand the space of responsible design that can actually be built.

</details>


### [56] [Beyond the Desk: Barriers and Future Opportunities for AI to Assist Scientists in Embodied Physical Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19504)
*Irene Hou,Alexander Qin,Lauren Cheng,Philip J. Guo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究首次探讨科学家如何在实验室和野外等物理环境中使用AI进行实体任务，发现三大采用障碍，并提出了未来AI助手的设计方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注科学家在"办公桌"上使用AI进行计算机工作，但科学工作经常发生在实验室和野外等"办公桌之外"的物理环境。需要了解科学家如何在实体物理任务中使用AI，以及面临的挑战和需求。

Method: 对12名从事实验室和野外工作的科学从业者进行访谈，涵盖核聚变、灵长类认知、生物化学等领域。通过定性分析识别AI采用障碍，并引导参与者设计未来AI助手的概念方案。

Result: 发现三个主要障碍：1)实验设置风险高，无法承受AI错误；2)受限环境难以使用AI；3)AI无法匹配人类的隐性知识。参与者设计了五种未来AI助手概念：任务状态监控、实验室知识组织、科学家健康监测、野外勘察、动手杂务处理。

Conclusion: AI应作为支持物理工作的背景基础设施，而非替代人类专业知识。未来AI助手设计应关注监控、组织、健康监测、勘察和杂务处理等支持性功能，以增强而非取代科学家的实体工作能力。

Abstract: More scientists are now using AI, but prior studies have examined only how they use it 'at the desk' for computer-based work. However, given that scientific work often happens 'beyond the desk' at lab and field sites, we conducted the first study of how scientific practitioners use AI for embodied physical tasks. We interviewed 12 scientific practitioners doing hands-on lab and fieldwork in domains like nuclear fusion, primate cognition, and biochemistry, and found three barriers to AI adoption in these settings: 1) experimental setups are too high-stakes to risk AI errors, 2) constrained environments make it hard to use AI, and 3) AI cannot match the tacit knowledge of humans. Participants then developed speculative designs for future AI assistants to 1) monitor task status, 2) organize lab-wide knowledge, 3) monitor scientists' health, 4) do field scouting, 5) do hands-on chores. Our findings point toward AI as background infrastructure to support physical work rather than replacing human expertise.

</details>


### [57] [Behavioral Engagement in VR-Based Sign Language Learning: Visual Attention as a Predictor of Performance and Temporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19535)
*Davide Traini,José Manuel Alcalde-Llergo,Mariana Buenestado-Fernández,Domenico Ursino,Enrique Yeguas-Bolívar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究分析了VR手语学习应用SONAR中的行为参与度指标（视觉注意力、视频回放频率、回放后观看时间）与学习表现的关系，发现视觉注意力和回放后观看时间是学习成功的显著预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索虚拟现实环境中行为参与度指标与学习表现之间的关系，特别是在手语学习这种需要高度视觉注意力的任务中，了解哪些行为指标能够有效预测学习效果。

Method: 采用自动推导的三个参与度指标：视觉注意力(VA)、视频回放频率(VRF)、回放后观看时间(PPVT)。参与者完成自定进度的训练阶段和验证测验。使用皮尔逊相关分析检验参与度指标与测验表现的关系，然后使用二项式广义线性模型回归评估它们的联合预测贡献。此外，通过聚合所有学习者的瞬时VA轨迹进行时间分析。

Result: VA与测验表现呈强正相关，PPVT次之，而VRF无显著关联。二项式GLM确认VA和PPVT是学习成功的显著预测因子，共同解释了大部分表现方差。时间分析显示注意力峰值与信息密集的视频片段对齐，并揭示了特定的参与动态模式：初始适应期、学习期间的振荡性注意力周期、评估期间的显著注意力峰值。

Conclusion: 研究强调了持续且策略性分配的视觉注意力在VR手语学习中的核心作用，证明了行为轨迹数据对于理解和预测沉浸式环境中学习者参与度的价值。这些发现为设计更有效的VR学习体验提供了实证依据。

Abstract: This study analyzes behavioral engagement in SONAR, a virtual reality application designed for sign language training and validation. We focus on three automatically derived engagement indicators (Visual Attention (VA), Video Replay Frequency (VRF), and Post-Playback Viewing Time (PPVT)) and examine their relationship with learning performance. Participants completed a self-paced Training phase, followed by a Validation quiz assessing retention. We employed Pearson correlation analysis to examine the relationships between engagement indicators and quiz performance, followed by binomial Generalized Linear Model (GLM) regression to assess their joint predictive contributions. Additionally, we conducted temporal analysis by aggregating moment-to-moment VA traces across all learners to characterize engagement dynamics during the learning session. Results show that VA exhibits a strong positive correlation with quiz performance,followed by PPVT, whereas VRF shows no meaningful association. A binomial GLM confirms that VA and PPVT are significant predictors of learning success, jointly explaining a substantial proportion of performance variance. Going beyond outcome-oriented analysis, we characterize temporal engagement patterns by aggregating moment-to-moment VA traces across all learners. The temporal profile reveals distinct attention peaks aligned with informationally dense segments of both training and validation videos, as well as phase-specific engagement dynamics, including initial acclimatization, oscillatory attention cycles during learning, and pronounced attentional peaks during assessment. Together, these findings highlight the central role of sustained and strategically allocated visual attention in VR-based sign language learning and demonstrate the value of behavioral trace data for understanding and predicting learner engagement in immersive environments.

</details>


### [58] [AI as Relational Translator: Rethinking Belonging and Mutual Legibility in Cross-Cultural Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19568)
*Yao Xiao,Rafael A. Calvo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该立场论文挑战"AI作为伴侣"范式，提出转向"AI支持人际关系"的新框架——关系AI翻译，将AI定位为文化关系基础设施，促进人类跨文化、代际和地理的连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AI伴侣使用可能加剧孤独感和减少线下社交的问题，挑战当前主流的"AI作为伴侣"范式，寻求更健康、可持续的AI支持人际关系的方式。

Method: 提出关系AI翻译框架，采用多智能体架构实现三种翻译操作：情感意图解码、情境重构和关系脚手架。以第一代东亚移民为关键案例进行理论分析。

Result: 构建了一个将AI定位为文化关系基础设施的理论框架，提出了围绕测量、安全架构以及技术干预与结构正义之间张力的设计挑战。

Conclusion: 成功应定义为向重新建立人与人之间支持的"毕业"，而非持续依赖系统。AI应从模拟人际关系转向支持人际关系，作为促进人类连接的桥梁而非替代品。

Abstract: Against rising global loneliness, AI companions promise connection, yet accumulating evidence suggests that, for some users and contexts, intensive companion-style use can correlate with increased loneliness and reduced offline socialisation. This position paper challenges the dominant "AI as companion" paradigm by proposing a shift: from AI that simulates relationships with humans to AI that supports relationships between humans. We introduce Relational AI Translation, positioning AI as cultural-relational infrastructure that scaffolds human connection across cultural, generational, and geographical divides. Using first-generation East Asian migrants as a theoretically productive critical case, we outline a multi-agent architecture instantiating three translation operations: emotion-intent decoding, contextual reframing, and relational scaffolding. We articulate design provocations around measurement, safety architecture, and the tension between technological intervention and structural justice, and explicitly frame success as graduation toward renewed human-to-human support rather than sustained engagement with the system.

</details>


### [59] [AI Psychosis: Does Conversational AI Amplify Delusion-Related Language?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19574)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Vipin Gunda,Jiwon Kim,Violeta J. Rodriguez,Hari Sundaram,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究表明，与对话AI的长期互动会加剧妄想相关语言，特别是对已有妄想倾向的用户，而基于妄想评分的状态感知机制可有效缓解这一风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话AI系统越来越多地用于个人反思和情感倾诉，引发了对其影响脆弱用户的担忧。近期轶事报告表明，与AI的长期互动可能强化妄想思维（有时称为"AI精神病"），但缺乏实证证据。本研究旨在探究妄想相关语言在与对话AI的多轮互动中如何演变。

Method: 从Reddit用户的纵向发帖历史构建模拟用户(SimUsers)，与三种模型家族(GPT、LLaMA、Qwen)生成扩展对话。开发DelusionScore（妄想评分）作为量化妄想相关语言强度的语言学指标，测量其在对话轮次中的变化轨迹。

Result: 有先前妄想相关话语的用户（处理组）表现出逐渐增加的DelusionScore轨迹，而无此类话语的用户（对照组）保持稳定或下降。放大效应在不同主题间存在差异，现实怀疑和强迫推理表现出最强增长。基于当前DelusionScore调整AI响应可显著降低这些轨迹。

Conclusion: 研究提供了实证证据，表明对话AI互动在长期使用中会放大妄想相关语言，强调了状态感知安全机制对于缓解此类风险的重要性。基于妄想评分的条件响应是有效的缓解策略。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems are increasingly used for personal reflection and emotional disclosure, raising concerns about their effects on vulnerable users. Recent anecdotal reports suggest that prolonged interactions with AI may reinforce delusional thinking -- a phenomenon sometimes described as AI Psychosis. However, empirical evidence on this phenomenon remains limited. In this work, we examine how delusion-related language evolves during multi-turn interactions with conversational AI. We construct simulated users (SimUsers) from Reddit users' longitudinal posting histories and generate extended conversations with three model families (GPT, LLaMA, and Qwen). We develop DelusionScore, a linguistic measure that quantifies the intensity of delusion-related language across conversational turns. We find that SimUsers derived from users with prior delusion-related discourse (Treatment) exhibit progressively increasing DelusionScore trajectories, whereas those derived from users without such discourse (Control) remain stable or decline. We further find that this amplification varies across themes, with reality skepticism and compulsive reasoning showing the strongest increases. Finally, conditioning AI responses on current DelusionScore substantially reduces these trajectories. These findings provide empirical evidence that conversational AI interactions can amplify delusion-related language over extended use and highlight the importance of state-aware safety mechanisms for mitigating such risks.

</details>


### [60] [HiFiGaze: Improving Eye Tracking Accuracy Using Screen Content Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19588)
*Taejun Kim,Vimal Mollyn,Riku Arakawa,Chris Harrison*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 利用设备屏幕在用户眼睛中的反射图像进行视线估计，通过已知的屏幕显示内容实现反射区域分割，从而准确追踪视线目标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费电子设备前置摄像头质量提升（如4K分辨率）使得能够捕捉屏幕在眼睛中的反射，但屏幕内容的多样性限制了传统方法的准确性。设备已知自身显示内容，这一信息可用于改进视线追踪

Method: 利用设备已知的屏幕显示内容信息，对眼睛中捕捉到的屏幕反射进行鲁棒分割，通过反射区域的位置和大小编码用户相对于屏幕的视线目标。探索了多种利用这一信号的策略

Result: 最佳模型相比基于外观的基线模型减少了约8%的平均追踪误差。补充研究表明，如果视线追踪摄像头位于设备底部，性能可再提升10-20%

Conclusion: 利用设备屏幕在眼睛中的反射信息，结合已知的屏幕显示内容，能够实现更准确的消费设备视线估计，摄像头位置对性能有显著影响

Abstract: We present a new and accurate approach for gaze estimation on consumer computing devices. We take advantage of continued strides in the quality of user-facing cameras found in e.g., smartphones, laptops, and desktops - 4K or greater in high-end devices - such that it is now possible to capture the 2D reflection of a device's screen in the user's eyes. This alone is insufficient for accurate gaze tracking due to the near-infinite variety of screen content. Crucially, however, the device knows what is being displayed on its own screen - in this work, we show this information allows for robust segmentation of the reflection, the location and size of which encodes the user's screen-relative gaze target. We explore several strategies to leverage this useful signal, quantifying performance in a user study. Our best performing model reduces mean tracking error by ~8% compared to a baseline appearance-based model. A supplemental study reveals an additional 10-20% improvement if the gaze-tracking camera is located at the bottom of the device.

</details>


### [61] [MetaCues: Enabling Critical Engagement with Generative AI for Information Seeking and Sensemaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19634)
*Anjali Singh,Karan Taneja,Zhitong Guan,Soo Young Rieh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MetaCues是一款集成元认知提示和笔记功能的GenAI搜索工具，旨在减少认知卸载，促进主动参与和批判性思维，提升信息搜索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GenAI搜索工具设计倾向于鼓励认知卸载，导致用户被动参与、选择性注意和信息同质化。有效使用需要元认知参与来制定优质提示、验证AI输出并批判性处理信息。

Method: 开发MetaCues工具，在AI响应旁提供元认知提示，并集成笔记界面以引导用户搜索和学习过程。通过在线研究(N=146)比较MetaCues与无提示基线工具，使用两个需要探索多元视角以形成知情判断的广泛搜索主题。

Result: 初步结果显示，MetaCues能增强用户对搜索主题态度判断的信心，并促进更广泛的探究。后一效果主要在争议性较小且用户相对不熟悉的主题中显现。

Conclusion: MetaCues通过元认知提示有效改善GenAI搜索中的认知参与度。未来需要进一步定性探索搜索交互和探究模式，以优化工具设计。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) search tools are increasingly used for information seeking, yet their design tends to encourage cognitive offloading, which may lead to passive engagement, selective attention, and informational homogenization. Effective use requires metacognitive engagement to craft good prompts, verify AI outputs, and critically engage with information. We developed MetaCues, a novel GenAI-based interactive tool for information seeking that delivers metacognitive cues alongside AI responses and a note-taking interface to guide users' search and associated learning. Through an online study (N = 146), we compared MetaCues to a baseline tool without cues, across two broad search topics that required participants to explore diverse perspectives in order to make informed judgments. Preliminary findings regarding participants' search behavior show that MetaCues leads to increased confidence in attitudinal judgments about the search topic as well as broader inquiry, with the latter effect emerging primarily for the topic that was less controversial and with which participants had relatively less familiarity. Accordingly, we outline directions for future qualitative exploration of search interactions and inquiry patterns.

</details>


### [62] [Sensing Your Vocals: Exploring the Activity of Vocal Cord Muscles for Pitch Assessment Using Electromyography and Ultrasonography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19698)
*Kanyu Chen,Rebecca Panskus,Erwin Wu,Yichen Peng,Daichi Saito,Emiko Kamiyama,Ruiteng Li,Chen-Chieh Liao,Karola Marky,Kato Akira,Hideki Koike,Kai Kunze*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探索使用肌电图（EMG）和超声成像（UI）可视化声乐训练中不可见的内部肌肉活动，通过三个实验分析不同水平歌手的肌肉控制差异，开发可视化系统，并与传统方法对比，为声乐肌肉训练系统提供改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声乐训练困难在于控制音高、共鸣和发声的内部肌肉对学习者不可见。研究旨在探索EMG和UI技术如何使这些肌肉活动可视化，从而改进声乐训练效果。

Method: 1. 对16名歌手（初学者、有经验者、专业者）进行EMG和UI数据分析，揭示三个声乐群体肌肉控制熟练度的差异。
2. 基于收集数据开发专家肌肉活动可视化参考系统，在12名新手用户研究中测试。
3. 通过焦点小组研究（15名有经验歌手）比较EMG/UI方法与传统的音频分析和教练指导方法。

Result: 1. EMG和UI数据揭示了三个声乐群体在肌肉控制熟练度上的显著差异。
2. 可视化系统测试显示：EMG能突出肌肉激活的细微差别，UI能提供声带长度和动态的洞察。
3. 与传统方法对比表明，EMG在改善声乐技能发展和增强反馈系统方面具有潜力。

Conclusion: 研究详细比较了EMG、UI和传统方法，为改进声乐肌肉训练系统提供了具体建议。EMG在声乐技能发展方面表现出前景，可视化技术能有效补充传统训练方法。

Abstract: Vocal training is difficult because the muscles that control pitch, resonance, and phonation are internal and invisible to learners. This paper investigates how Electromyography (EMG) and ultrasonic imaging (UI) can make these muscles observable for training purposes. We report three studies. First, we analyze the EMG and UI data from 16 singers (beginners, experienced & professionals), revealing differences among three vocal groups of the muscle control proficiency. Second, we use the collected data to create a system that visualizes an expert's muscle activity as reference. This system is tested in a user study with 12 novices, showing that EMG highlighted muscle activation nuances, while UI provided insights into vocal cord length and dynamics. Third, to compare our approach to traditional methods (audio analysis and coach instructions), we conducted a focus group study with 15 experienced singers. Our results suggest that EMG is promising for improving vocal skill development and enhancing feedback systems. We conclude the paper with a detailed comparison of the analyzed modalities (EMG, UI and traditional methods), resulting in recommendations to improve vocal muscle training systems.

</details>


### [63] [Abstraction Beats Realism: Physiological Visualizations Enhance Arousal Synchrony in VR Concert Recreations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19730)
*Xiaru Meng,Yulan Ju,Yan He,Matthias Hoppe,Kouta Minamizawa,Jiawen Han,Kai Kunze*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 抽象生理可视化比写实VR视频更能重现现场音乐会观众的生理同步性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟现实文化体验需要重现现场观众的集体生理共振，但现有VR评估方法依赖事后自我报告，会打断沉浸感且无法捕捉实时生理动态

Method: 提出跨时程生理同步性作为无干扰评估方法：测量VR参与者的生理唤醒模式与原始现场观众的匹配程度。分两阶段研究：1) 记录40名现场音乐会观众的皮肤电活动；2) 创建三种不同抽象程度的VR重现（写实360度视频、混合视频+可视化、完全抽象生理表示），使用动态时间规整测量22名实验室参与者的同步性

Result: 与偏向写实性的假设相反，抽象可视化实现了与现场观众最强的同步性。在音乐高潮部分，抽象条件保持相关性，而写实视频则没有相关性

Conclusion: 抽象生理表示可能比写实视频更能有效唤起VR文化重现中的真实集体参与感，为评估VR文化体验提供了无干扰的生理同步性方法

Abstract: Live cultural experiences like concerts generate shared physiological arousal among audience members, a collective resonance that contributes to their emotional power. Recreating such experiences in virtual reality therefore requires not just audiovisual fidelity, but reproduction of this physiological dimension. Yet current VR evaluation methods rely on post-hoc self-reports that interrupt immersion and cannot capture moment-to-moment arousal dynamics. We propose cross-temporal physiological synchrony as an unobtrusive methodology for evaluating VR cultural recreations: measuring how closely a VR participant's arousal patterns align with those of the original live audience. In a two-phase study, we recorded electrodermal activity from 40 live concert attendees, then created three VR recreations with varying abstraction levels (realistic 360-degree video, mixed video-plus-visualization, and fully abstract physiological representations) and measured synchrony with 22 laboratory participants using Dynamic Time Warping. Contrary to assumptions favoring realism, abstract visualizations achieved the strongest synchrony with live audiences. During musical climaxes, the abstract condition maintained correlation while realistic video showed none. These findings suggest that abstract physiological representations may be more effective than realistic footage for evoking authentic collective engagement in VR cultural recreations.

</details>


### [64] [ConSearcher: Supporting Conversational Information Seeking in Online Communities with Member Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19747)
*Shiwei Wu,Xinyue Chen,Yuheng Liu,Xingbo Wang,Qingyu Guo,Longfei Chen,Chuhan Shi,Zhenhui Peng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出ConSearcher，一个基于LLM的对话搜索工具，通过动态生成社区成员角色来促进在线社区中的信息寻求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多人在在线社区中浏览他人经验和观点以获取信息（如制定旅行计划），但基于大语言模型的对话搜索在在线社区中的应用仍研究不足

Method: 1. 先导研究(N=10)评估经典对话搜索工具的有效性并识别改进空间；2. 提出ConSearcher工具，基于用户查询动态生成成员角色，用户可通过检查类似自己的模拟成员可能提出的问题来澄清兴趣，并从不同成员视角获取响应；3. 采用组内设计研究(N=27)比较ConSearcher与两个对话搜索基线

Result: 与两个对话搜索基线相比，ConSearcher显著提高了信息寻求结果和用户参与度，但也引发了过度个性化的担忧

Conclusion: ConSearcher展示了在在线社区中支持对话式信息寻求的潜力，但需要平衡个性化与多样性，讨论了在在线社区中支持对话信息寻求的启示

Abstract: Many people browse online communities to learn from others' experiences and opinions, e.g., for constructing travel plans. Conversational search powered by large language models (LLMs) could ease this information-seeking task, but it remains under-investigated within the online community. In this paper, we first conducted an exploratory study (N=10) that indicated the helpfulness of a classic conversational search tool and identified room for improvement. Then, we proposed ConSearcher, an LLM-powered tool with dynamically generated member personas based on user queries to facilitate conversational search in the community. In ConSearcher, users can clarify their interests by checking what a simulated member similar to them may ask and get responses from diverse members' perspectives. A within-subjects study (N=27) showed that compared to two conversational search baselines, ConSearcher led to significantly higher information-seeking outcome and user engagement but raised concerns about over-personalization. We discuss implications for supporting conversational information seeking in online communities.

</details>


### [65] [Sense4HRI: A ROS 2 HRI Framework for Physiological Sensor Integration and Synchronized Logging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19914)
*Manuel Scheibl,Julian Leichert,Sinem Görmez,Britta Wrede*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Sense4HRI：一个基于ROS 2的可扩展人机交互框架，用于集成生理信号测量和用户状态评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ROS 2人机交互框架缺乏标准化的生理信号集成支持，而生理信号对评估用户心理状态日益重要

Method: 开发Sense4HRI框架，提供时间戳生理时间序列数据的可重用接口，支持生理传感器集成、信号解释和多模态融合，并实现生理信号与实验上下文的同步记录

Result: 创建了一个可扩展的ROS 2框架，支持标准化生理数据流集成，实现可互操作和可追溯的多模态分析

Conclusion: Sense4HRI填补了ROS 2人机交互框架中生理信号集成标准化的空白，为稳健评估用户心理状态提供了可扩展解决方案

Abstract: Physiological signals are increasingly relevant to estimate the mental states of users in human-robot interaction (HRI), yet ROS 2-based HRI frameworks still lack reusable support to integrate such data streams in a standardized way. Therefore, we propose Sense4HRI, an adapted framework for human-robot interaction in ROS 2 that integrates physiological measurements and derived user-state indicators. The framework is designed to be extensible, allowing the integration of additional physiological sensors, their interpretation, and multimodal fusion to provide a robust assessment of the mental states of users. In addition, it introduces reusable interfaces for timestamped physiological time-series data and supports synchronized logging of physiological signals together with experiment context, enabling interoperable and traceable multimodal analysis within ROS 2-based HRI systems.

</details>


### [66] [Promoting Critical Thinking With Domain-Specific Generative AI Provocations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19975)
*Thomas Şerban von Davier,Hao-Ping Lee,Jodi Forlizzi,Sauvik Das*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨生成式AI对批判性思维的混合影响，通过两个工具案例（ArtBot和Privy）分析设计决策如何影响AI驱动挑衅的有效性，发现领域特定挑衅、生产性摩擦和用户贡献依赖的交互能有效支持批判性思维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对生成式AI如何影响批判性思维存在矛盾结论，既有潜在危害也有益处，取决于具体实施方式。研究旨在探索AI驱动的挑衅（如要求人类澄清和论证的问题）如何有效激发批判性思维，并通过实际工具设计经验来理解设计决策如何塑造这类挑衅的形式和效果。

Method: 基于设计和评估两个生成式AI工具的经验：ArtBot（美术解释领域）和Privy（AI隐私领域）。通过观察和用户反馈分析设计决策如何影响挑衅的形式和有效性，探讨领域特定挑衅、生产性摩擦以及依赖用户贡献的交互设计。

Result: 研究发现领域特定的挑衅、通过生产性摩擦实现的实施方式，以及依赖用户贡献的交互设计能够有意义地支持批判性思维。参与者的体验表明，支持批判性思维可能需要超越静态挑衅，转向适应用户偏好和专业知识水平的方法。

Conclusion: 生成式AI对批判性思维的影响取决于具体设计决策。有效的AI驱动挑衅需要领域特定性、生产性摩擦和用户贡献依赖的交互。未来设计应超越静态挑衅，发展能够适应用户偏好和专业知识水平的自适应方法，以更好地支持批判性思维发展。

Abstract: The evidence on the effects of generative AI (GenAI) on critical thinking is mixed, with studies suggesting both potential harms and benefits depending on its implementation. Some argue that AI-driven provocations, such as questions asking for human clarification and justification, are beneficial for eliciting critical thinking. Drawing on our experience designing and evaluating two GenAI-powered tools for knowledge work, ArtBot in the domain of fine art interpretation and Privy in the domain of AI privacy, we reflect on how design decisions shape the form and effectiveness of such provocations. Our observations and user feedback suggest that domain-specific provocations, implemented through productive friction and interactions that depend on user contribution, can meaningfully support critical thinking. We present participant experiences with both prototypes and discuss how supporting critical thinking may require moving beyond static provocations toward approaches that adapt to user preferences and levels of expertise.

</details>


### [67] [Demonstration of Adapt4Me: An Uncertainty-Aware Authoring Environment for Personalizing Automatic Speech Recognition to Non-normative Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20112)
*Niclas Pokel,Yiming Zhao,Pehuén Moure,Yingqiang Gao,Roman Böhringer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Adapt4Me是一个基于Web的去中心化环境，通过贝叶斯主动学习和人机交互工作流，使非专业用户能够端到端地个性化非规范性语音的自动语音识别模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非规范性语音的自动语音识别个性化面临数据收集劳动密集和技术复杂两大挑战，需要降低专业门槛，让用户能够自主参与模型优化。

Method: 采用三阶段人机交互工作流：1) 通过贪婪音素采样快速分析说话者特定声学特征；2) 使用变分推断低秩适应(VI-LoRA)进行后端个性化，支持快速增量更新；3) 通过可视化模型不确定性和低摩擦top-k修正实现持续改进。

Result: Adapt4Me将认知不确定性显式化，将数据效率重构为交互设计特征而非纯算法问题，使用户能够个性化鲁棒的ASR模型，从被动数据源转变为辅助技术的主动创作者。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种去中心化的个性化ASR框架，通过贝叶斯主动学习和人机交互设计，使非专业用户能够端到端地优化非规范性语音识别模型，降低了技术门槛。

Abstract: Personalizing Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) for non-normative speech remains challenging because data collection is labor-intensive and model training is technically complex. To address these limitations, we propose Adapt4Me, a web-based decentralized environment that operationalizes Bayesian active learning to enable end-to-end personalization without expert supervision. The app exposes data selection, adaptation, and validation to lay users through a three-stage human-in-the-loop workflow: (1) rapid profiling via greedy phoneme sampling to capture speaker-specific acoustics; (2) backend personalization using Variational Inference Low-Rank Adaptation (VI-LoRA) to enable fast, incremental updates; and (3) continuous improvement, where users guide model refinement by resolving visualized model uncertainty via low-friction top-k corrections. By making epistemic uncertainty explicit, Adapt4Me reframes data efficiency as an interactive design feature rather than a purely algorithmic concern. We show how this enables users to personalize robust ASR models, transforming them from passive data sources into active authors of their own assistive technology.

</details>

### [68] [CDEoH: Category-Driven Automatic Algorithm Design With Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19284)
*Yu-Nian Wang,Shen-Huan Lyu,Ning Chen,Jia-Le Xu,Baoliu Ye,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: CDEoH是一种基于大语言模型的自动算法设计方法，通过显式建模算法类别并平衡性能与类别多样性，解决现有LLM启发式搜索方法中的不稳定和早熟收敛问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的启发式搜索方法在自动算法生成中表现出强大能力，但其进化过程常存在不稳定和早熟收敛问题。现有方法主要通过提示工程或联合进化思想和代码来解决，但忽视了算法类别多样性对维持进化稳定性的关键作用。

Method: 提出CDEoH方法，显式建模算法类别，在种群管理中联合平衡性能和类别多样性，实现跨多个算法范式的并行探索。

Result: 在多个尺度的代表性组合优化问题上进行广泛实验，CDEoH有效缓解了向单一进化方向的收敛，显著增强了进化稳定性，在任务和尺度上实现了一致优越的平均性能。

Conclusion: CDEoH通过显式考虑算法类别多样性，解决了LLM启发式搜索中的进化稳定性问题，为自动算法设计提供了更稳定和有效的框架。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), LLM-based heuristic search methods have demonstrated strong capabilities in automated algorithm generation. However, their evolutionary processes often suffer from instability and premature convergence. Existing approaches mainly address this issue through prompt engineering or by jointly evolving thought and code, while largely overlooking the critical role of algorithmic category diversity in maintaining evolutionary stability. To this end, we propose Category Driven Automatic Algorithm Design with Large Language Models (CDEoH), which explicitly models algorithm categories and jointly balances performance and category diversity in population management, enabling parallel exploration across multiple algorithmic paradigms. Extensive experiments on representative combinatorial optimization problems across multiple scales demonstrate that CDEoH effectively mitigates convergence toward a single evolutionary direction, significantly enhancing evolutionary stability and achieving consistently superior average performance across tasks and scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [69] [Federated Hyperdimensional Computing for Resource-Constrained Industrial IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20037)
*Nikita Zeulin,Olga Galinina,Nageen Himayat,Sergey Andreev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将超维计算（HDC）集成到联邦学习（FL）框架中，为资源受限的工业物联网（IIoT）提供轻量级、通信高效的分布式智能解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网设备在内存、计算能力和无线带宽方面存在严格限制，这给预测性和规范性维护等高级数据分析任务的部署带来了挑战。需要为资源受限的IIoT环境寻找轻量级的学习范式。

Method: 将超维计算（HDC）集成到联邦学习（FL）框架中，设备仅交换原型表示而非原始数据或完整模型参数，显著降低通信开销。利用高维向量空间的特性实现高效训练和推理。

Result: 数值结果表明，联邦HDC在IIoT中支持协作学习，具有快速收敛速度和通信效率。HDC代表了大规模、资源受限IIoT环境中轻量级且具有弹性的分布式智能框架。

Conclusion: 超维计算作为轻量级学习范式，结合联邦学习框架，能够有效解决工业物联网在资源受限条件下的分布式智能部署问题，实现通信高效的协作学习。

Abstract: In the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) systems, edge devices often operate under strict constraints in memory, compute capability, and wireless bandwidth. These limitations challenge the deployment of advanced data analytics tasks, such as predictive and prescriptive maintenance. In this work, we explore hyperdimensional computing (HDC) as a lightweight learning paradigm for resource-constrained IIoT. Conventional centralized HDC leverages the properties of high-dimensional vector spaces to enable energy-efficient training and inference. We integrate this paradigm into a federated learning (FL) framework where devices exchange only prototype representations, which significantly reduces communication overhead. Our numerical results highlight the potential of federated HDC to support collaborative learning in IIoT with fast convergence speed and communication efficiency. These results indicate that HDC represents a lightweight and resilient framework for distributed intelligence in large-scale and resource-constrained IIoT environments.

</details>


### [70] [Speculating Experts Accelerates Inference for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19289)
*Vivan Madan,Prajwal Singhania,Abhinav Bhatele,Tom Goldstein,Ashwinee Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出专家预取方案，利用模型内部表示预测未来专家，实现CPU-GPU传输与计算重叠，减少MoE模型在内存受限推理时的性能瓶颈


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在内存受限推理时，专家权重需卸载到CPU，导致CPU-GPU传输成为解码性能瓶颈，需要解决计算与内存传输的同步问题

Method: 1) 利用当前计算的内部模型表示预测未来专家，实现专家预取；2) 执行推测的专家以保持任务精度；3) 对推测执行精度不佳的情况，引入轻量级估计器提高专家预测命中率

Result: 1) 证明内部表示可可靠预测未来专家；2) 推测执行通常能保持下游任务精度；3) 在优化推理引擎中实现TPOT降低达14%；4) 轻量级估计器可减少性能退化

Conclusion: 专家预取方案有效缓解MoE模型在内存受限推理时的CPU-GPU传输瓶颈，通过计算与内存传输重叠显著提升推理效率，且开源实现可供社区使用

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have gained popularity as a means of scaling the capacity of large language models (LLMs) while maintaining sparse activations and reduced per-token compute. However, in memory-constrained inference settings, expert weights must be offloaded to CPU, creating a performance bottleneck from CPU-GPU transfers during decoding. We propose an expert prefetching scheme that leverages currently computed internal model representations to speculate future experts, enabling memory transfers to overlap with computation. Across multiple MoE architectures, we demonstrate that future experts can be reliably predicted by these internal representations. We also demonstrate that executing speculated experts generally maintains downstream task accuracy, thus preserving more effective compute-memory overlap by eliminating the need to re-fetch true router-selected experts. Integrated into an optimized inference engine, our approach achieves up to 14\% reduction in time per output token (TPOT) over on-demand loading of experts from CPU memory. For MoEs where speculative execution alone yields suboptimal accuracy, we further examine lightweight estimators that improve expert prediction hit rates, thereby reducing performance degradation. Our code is released in open-source at https://github.com/axonn-ai/yalis/tree/offload_prefetch.

</details>


### [71] [A Visualization for Comparative Analysis of Regression Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19291)
*Nassime Mountasir,Baptiste Lafabregue,Bruno Albert,Nicolas Lachiche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的回归模型性能可视化方法，通过二维残差空间、马氏距离和百分位色彩映射来更全面地评估和比较模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归性能指标（如MAE、RMSE、R²）虽然广泛使用，但过度聚合信息，无法揭示误差分布模式和模型间的细微差异，需要更细致的可视化方法来深入理解模型性能

Method: 基于三个主要贡献：(1) 在二维空间中考虑残差，同时评估两个模型的误差；(2) 利用马氏距离考虑数据中的相关性和尺度差异；(3) 使用色彩映射可视化基于百分位的误差分布，便于识别密集区域和异常值

Result: 该方法能够图形化表示误差分布及其相关性，提供比传统聚合指标更详细全面的模型性能视图，帮助用户发现传统方法可能掩盖的模式

Conclusion: 提出的可视化方法促进了对回归模型性能差异和误差分布的深入理解，增强了评估和比较过程，为回归模型性能分析提供了更强大的工具

Abstract: As regression is a widely studied problem, many methods have been proposed to solve it, each of them often requiring setting different hyper-parameters. Therefore, selecting the proper method for a given application may be very difficult and relies on comparing their performances. Performance is usually measured using various metrics such as Mean Absolute Error (MAE), Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE), or R-squared (R${}^2$). These metrics provide a numerical summary of predictive accuracy by quantifying the difference between predicted and actual values. However, while these metrics are widely used in the literature for summarizing model performance and useful to distinguish between models performing poorly and well, they often aggregate too much information. This article addresses these limitations by introducing a novel visualization approach that highlights key aspects of regression model performance. The proposed method builds upon three main contributions: (1) considering the residuals in a 2D space, which allows for simultaneous evaluation of errors from two models, (2) leveraging the Mahalanobis distance to account for correlations and differences in scale within the data, and (3) employing a colormap to visualize the percentile-based distribution of errors, making it easier to identify dense regions and outliers. By graphically representing the distribution of errors and their correlations, this approach provides a more detailed and comprehensive view of model performance, enabling users to uncover patterns that traditional aggregate metrics may obscure. The proposed visualization method facilitates a deeper understanding of regression model performance differences and error distributions, enhancing the evaluation and comparison process.

</details>


### [72] [Maximizing mutual information between user-contexts and responses improve LLM personalization with no additional data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19294)
*Hyunji Nam,Haoran Li,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIPO是一种通过对比数据增强构建偏好对的自改进框架，无需外部监督即可提升LLM在个性化和推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法严重依赖人工标注数据或外部验证器，高质量数据收集成本高昂，且真正智能超越易验证任务范畴，需要无需外部监督的自改进框架。

Method: 提出互信息偏好优化(MIPO)：通过基于正确提示生成正响应、基于随机无关提示生成负响应来构建对比偏好对，使用DPO从该配对数据中学习，最大化提示与模型响应之间的点条件互信息。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen-Instruct模型上的实验显示：用于最大化用户上下文与响应间互信息时，MIPO在真实用户数据集上实现3-40%的个性化任务改进；在数学和多选题任务上实现1-18%提升，且无需额外数据或人工监督。

Conclusion: MIPO展示了无需外部监督的自改进潜力，为LLM自我提升提供了有前景的方向，特别是在个性化和推理任务上表现出色。

Abstract: While post-training has successfully improved large language models (LLMs) across a variety of domains, these gains heavily rely on human-labeled data or external verifiers. Existing data has already been exploited, and new high-quality data is expensive to collect. More fundamentally, true intelligence goes far beyond tasks that are easily verifiable. Therefore, we need self-improvement frameworks that allow models to improve without external oversight. We propose *Mutual Information Preference Optimization (MIPO)*, a contrastive data augmentation method that constructs preference pairs by generating a positive response conditioning on the correct prompt, and a negative response by conditioning on a random, unrelated prompt. We show that using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to learn from this paired data maximizes pointwise conditional mutual information (MI) (under the base LLM) between prompts and model responses. Empirical results with various-sized Llama- and Qwen-Instruct models show that when used to maximize MI between user context and response, MIPO provides an effective personalization technique, achieving 3-40% improvements on personalization tasks using real-user datasets compared to strong baselines. Surprisingly, MIPO can also be applied to improve performance on math and multiple-choice problems, yielding 1-18% **without any additional data or human supervision**. These results suggest a promising direction for self-improvement.

</details>


### [73] [Heavy-Tailed and Long-Range Dependent Noise in Stochastic Approximation: A Finite-Time Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19648)
*Siddharth Chandak,Anuj Yadav,Ayfer Ozgur,Nicholas Bambos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在重尾和长程依赖噪声下的随机逼近算法，首次建立了这两种非经典噪声模型下的有限时间矩界，并量化了重尾和时间依赖对收敛速度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机逼近分析通常依赖于鞅差或具有有界二阶矩的马尔可夫噪声，但实际应用中（如金融和通信领域）经常遇到重尾和长程依赖噪声。现有理论无法充分处理这些非经典噪声模型，需要建立新的分析框架。

Method: 采用噪声平均化论证技术，在不修改迭代过程的情况下正则化噪声影响。研究强单调算子求根问题的随机逼近算法，分析重尾和长程依赖噪声下的收敛行为。

Result: 首次建立了重尾和长程依赖噪声下的有限时间矩界，提供了显式收敛速率，量化了重尾和时间依赖对收敛的影响。将一般框架应用于随机梯度下降和梯度博弈，并通过数值实验验证了有限时间分析。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了随机逼近理论，使其能够处理实际应用中常见的非经典噪声模型，为在重尾和长程依赖噪声环境下的优化和强化学习算法提供了理论基础和收敛保证。

Abstract: Stochastic approximation (SA) is a fundamental iterative framework with broad applications in reinforcement learning and optimization. Classical analyses typically rely on martingale difference or Markov noise with bounded second moments, but many practical settings, including finance and communications, frequently encounter heavy-tailed and long-range dependent (LRD) noise. In this work, we study SA for finding the root of a strongly monotone operator under these non-classical noise models. We establish the first finite-time moment bounds in both settings, providing explicit convergence rates that quantify the impact of heavy tails and temporal dependence. Our analysis employs a noise-averaging argument that regularizes the impact of noise without modifying the iteration. Finally, we apply our general framework to stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and gradient play, and corroborate our finite-time analysis through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [74] [BrainSCL: Subtype-Guided Contrastive Learning for Brain Disorder Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19295)
*Xiaolong Li,Guiliang Guo,Guangqi Wen,Peng Cao,Jinzhu Yang,Honglin Wu,Xiaoli Liu,Fei Wang,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种亚型引导的对比学习框架，通过建模患者异质性为潜在亚型，并将其作为结构先验来指导判别性表征学习，在精神障碍诊断中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神障碍人群存在显著的异质性，这给对比学习中正样本对的构建带来挑战。传统方法难以有效处理这种样本间差异，需要新的框架来建模患者异质性并指导表征学习。

Method: 1) 构建多视图表征：结合临床文本和从BOLD信号自适应学习的图结构；2) 无监督谱聚类发现潜在亚型；3) 提出双级注意力机制构建原型，捕获稳定的亚型特异性连接模式；4) 提出亚型引导的对比学习策略，将样本拉向其亚型原型图，增强亚型内一致性。

Result: 在重度抑郁症、双相情感障碍和自闭症谱系障碍上评估，实验结果表明亚型原型图能有效指导对比学习，所提方法优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过建模患者异质性为潜在亚型并作为结构先验，提出的亚型引导对比学习框架能有效处理精神障碍人群的异质性，提升模型性能，为精神障碍诊断提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mental disorder populations exhibit pronounced heterogeneity -- that is, the significant differences between samples -- poses a significant challenge to the definition of positive pairs in contrastive learning. To address this, we propose a subtype-guided contrastive learning framework that models patient heterogeneity as latent subtypes and incorporates them as structural priors to guide discriminative representation learning. Specifically, we construct multi-view representations by combining patients' clinical text with graph structure adaptively learned from BOLD signals, to uncover latent subtypes via unsupervised spectral clustering. A dual-level attention mechanism is proposed to construct prototypes for capturing stable subtype-specific connectivity patterns. We further propose a subtype-guided contrastive learning strategy that pulls samples toward their subtype prototype graph, reinforcing intra-subtype consistency for providing effective supervisory signals to improve model performance. We evaluate our method on Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), Bipolar Disorder (BD), and Autism Spectrum Disorders (ASD). Experimental results confirm the effectiveness of subtype prototype graphs in guiding contrastive learning and demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art approaches. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BrainSCL-06D7.

</details>


### [75] [GoAgent: Group-of-Agents Communication Topology Generation for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19677)
*Hongjiang Chen,Xin Zheng,Yixin Liu,Pengfei Jiao,Shiyuan Li,Huan Liu,Zhidong Zhao,Ziqi Xu,Ibrahim Khalil,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GoAgent提出了一种基于组的通信拓扑生成方法，将协作组作为多智能体系统构建的基本单元，通过条件信息瓶颈压缩组间通信，在减少17%令牌消耗的同时达到93.84%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统大多采用节点中心的方式生成通信拓扑，让组结构从局部连接决策中隐式出现，这往往导致次优协调和不必要的通信开销。需要一种能够显式建模协作组作为原子单元的方法。

Method: GoAgent首先通过LLM枚举任务相关的候选组，然后自回归地选择和连接这些组作为原子单元来构建最终通信图，同时捕捉组内凝聚力和组间协调。引入条件信息瓶颈目标来压缩组间通信，保留任务相关信号同时过滤冗余历史噪声。

Result: 在六个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，GoAgent实现了最先进的性能，平均准确率达到93.84%，同时减少了约17%的令牌消耗。

Conclusion: 将协作组作为原子单元显式建模，结合条件信息瓶颈压缩通信，能够有效提升多智能体系统的协调效率，在保持高性能的同时显著降低通信开销。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in solving complex tasks, yet their effectiveness depends heavily on the underlying communication topology that coordinates agent interactions. Within these systems, successful problem-solving often necessitates task-specific group structures to divide and conquer subtasks. However, most existing approaches generate communication topologies in a node-centric manner, leaving group structures to emerge implicitly from local connectivity decisions rather than modeling them explicitly, often leading to suboptimal coordination and unnecessary communication overhead. To address this limitation, we propose GoAgent (Group-of-Agents), a communication topology generation method that explicitly treats collaborative groups as the atomic units of MAS construction. Specifically, GoAgent first enumerates task-relevant candidate groups through an LLM and then autoregressively selects and connects these groups as atomic units to construct the final communication graph, jointly capturing intra-group cohesion and inter-group coordination. To mitigate communication redundancy and noise propagation inherent in expanding topologies, we further introduce a conditional information bottleneck (CIB) objective that compresses inter-group communication, preserving task-relevant signals while filtering out redundant historical noise. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of GoAgent with 93.84% average accuracy while reducing token consumption by about 17%.

</details>


### [76] [TTQ: Activation-Aware Test-Time Quantization to Accelerate LLM Inference On The Fly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19296)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Jing Liu,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出测试时量化框架，在推理时动态压缩大模型，解决传统激活感知量化方法因依赖校准数据而产生的领域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型基础模型的计算需求巨大，现有的无需重新训练的激活感知压缩技术高度依赖校准数据，在处理未见的下游任务时会出现领域偏移问题。

Method: 提出测试时量化框架，通过高效的在线校准，在推理时即时进行激活感知量化，能够适应每个提示而不受下游任务限制，同时实现推理加速。

Result: 多项实验表明，TTQ在量化性能上优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 测试时量化框架能够有效解决领域偏移问题，实现动态模型压缩，在保持推理加速的同时提升量化性能。

Abstract: To tackle the huge computational demand of large foundation models, activation-aware compression techniques without retraining have been introduced. However, since these methods highly rely on calibration data, domain shift issues may arise for unseen downstream tasks. We propose a test-time quantization (TTQ) framework which compresses large models on the fly at inference time to resolve this issue. With an efficient online calibration, instant activation-aware quantization can adapt every prompt regardless of the downstream tasks, yet achieving inference speedup. Several experiments demonstrate that TTQ can improve the quantization performance over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [77] [MeanFlow Meets Control: Scaling Sampled-Data Control for Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20189)
*Anqi Dong,Yongxin Chen,Karl H. Johansson,Johan Karlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于线性时不变动态下群体控制的控制空间学习框架，通过有限时间窗口控制量参数化，实现采样数据系统中的少步控制更新


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模群体控制在采样数据系统中面临挑战，因为控制输入是间歇更新并在有限区间内应用的。传统瞬时速度场方法不适用于实际采样数据系统，需要一种尊重系统采样数据结构的控制方法

Method: 引入控制空间学习框架，学习参数化每个采样区间内有限时域最小能量控制的系数。该系数具有积分表示和沿桥轨迹的局部微分恒等式，采用简单的停止梯度训练目标。学习到的系数直接在采样数据更新中使用，确保规定的动态和驱动映射得到遵守

Result: 该框架提供了一种可扩展的少步群体控制方法，与真实控制系统的采样数据结构保持一致，能够有效处理大规模群体在采样数据系统中的控制问题

Conclusion: 提出的控制空间学习框架为线性时不变动态下的群体控制提供了一种与采样数据结构一致的有效方法，通过有限时间窗口控制量参数化实现了可扩展的少步控制

Abstract: Steering large-scale swarms in only a few control updates is challenging because real systems operate in sampled-data form: control inputs are updated intermittently and applied over finite intervals. In this regime, the natural object is not an instantaneous velocity field, but a finite-window control quantity that captures the system response over each sampling interval. Inspired by MeanFlow, we introduce a control-space learning framework for swarm steering under linear time-invariant dynamics. The learned object is the coefficient that parameterizes the finite-horizon minimum-energy control over each interval. We show that this coefficient admits both an integral representation and a local differential identity along bridge trajectories, which leads to a simple stop-gradient training objective. At implementation time, the learned coefficient is used directly in sampled-data updates, so the prescribed dynamics and actuation map are respected by construction. The resulting framework provides a scalable approach to few-step swarm steering that is consistent with the sampled-data structure of real control systems.

</details>


### [78] [CLaRE-ty Amid Chaos: Quantifying Representational Entanglement to Predict Ripple Effects in LLM Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19297)
*Manit Baser,Alperen Yildiz,Dinil Mon Divakaran,Mohan Gurusamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLaRE是一种轻量级的表示级技术，用于识别模型编辑中的涟漪效应，通过前向激活量化事实间的纠缠关系，无需反向传播，显著提升了检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的静态知识表示会随时间过时或错误，模型编辑技术虽能修改事实关联但会产生不可预测的涟漪效应（传播到隐藏空间的行为变化），需要有效识别这些效应。

Method: CLaRE使用单一中间层的前向激活量化事实间的纠缠关系，避免了昂贵的反向传播。构建了包含11,427个事实的语料库，计算大规模纠缠图来捕捉局部编辑在表示空间中的传播。

Result: 与基线相比，CLaRE在Spearman相关性上平均提升62.2%，速度快2.74倍，峰值GPU内存使用减少2.85倍，存储需求也大幅降低。纠缠图可用于模型编辑的保留集、审计追踪、高效红队测试和可扩展的后编辑评估。

Conclusion: CLaRE提供了一种高效、轻量级的方法来识别模型编辑中的涟漪效应，通过前向激活量化事实纠缠，为模型编辑的可靠性和可扩展评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: The static knowledge representations of large language models (LLMs) inevitably become outdated or incorrect over time. While model-editing techniques offer a promising solution by modifying a model's factual associations, they often produce unpredictable ripple effects, which are unintended behavioral changes that propagate even to the hidden space. In this work, we introduce CLaRE, a lightweight representation-level technique to identify where these ripple effects may occur. Unlike prior gradient-based methods, CLaRE quantifies entanglement between facts using forward activations from a single intermediate layer, avoiding costly backward passes. To enable systematic study, we prepare and analyse a corpus of 11,427 facts drawn from three existing datasets. Using CLaRE, we compute large-scale entanglement graphs of this corpus for multiple models, capturing how local edits propagate through representational space. These graphs enable stronger preservation sets for model editing, audit trails, efficient red-teaming, and scalable post-edit evaluation. In comparison to baselines, CLaRE achieves an average of 62.2% improvement in Spearman correlation with ripple effects while being $2.74\times$ faster, and using $2.85\times$ less peak GPU memory. Besides, CLaRE requires only a fraction of the storage needed by the baselines to compute and preserve fact representations. Our entanglement graphs and corpus are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CLaRE-488E.

</details>


### [79] [A Super Fast K-means for Indexing Vector Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20009)
*Leonardo Kuffo,Sven Hepkema,Peter Boncz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SuperKMeans是一种针对高维向量嵌入聚类的k-means变体，在CPU上比FAISS和Scikit-Learn快7倍，在GPU上比cuVS快4倍，同时保持向量相似性搜索的聚类质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维向量嵌入的聚类需求，现有k-means实现存在数据访问和计算开销大的问题，需要更高效的聚类算法来加速向量相似性搜索任务。

Method: 1. 通过可靠高效地剪枝不需要的维度来减少数据访问和计算开销；2. 提出"基于召回率的提前终止"机制，当检索任务的聚类中心质量停止改善时提前终止迭代。

Result: 在CPU上比FAISS和Scikit-Learn快7倍，在GPU上比cuVS快4倍，同时保持向量检索质量；提前终止机制进一步减少运行时间而不影响检索质量。

Conclusion: SuperKMeans通过维度剪枝和提前终止机制显著加速高维向量嵌入的聚类，为向量相似性搜索任务提供高效解决方案，已开源实现。

Abstract: We present SuperKMeans: a k-means variant designed for clustering collections of high-dimensional vector embeddings. SuperKMeans' clustering is up to 7x faster than FAISS and Scikit-Learn on modern CPUs and up to 4x faster than cuVS on GPUs (Figure 1), while maintaining the quality of the resulting centroids for vector similarity search tasks. SuperKMeans acceleration comes from reducing data-access and compute overhead by reliably and efficiently pruning dimensions that are not needed to assign a vector to a centroid. Furthermore, we present Early Termination by Recall, a novel mechanism that early-terminates k-means when the quality of the centroids for retrieval tasks stops improving across iterations. In practice, this further reduces runtimes without compromising retrieval quality. We open-source our implementation at https://github.com/cwida/SuperKMeans

</details>


### [80] [PRIME-CVD: A Parametrically Rendered Informatics Medical Environment for Education in Cardiovascular Risk Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19299)
*Nicholas I-Hsien Kuo,Marzia Hoque Tania,Blanca Gallego,Louisa Jorm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRIME-CVD是一个参数化生成的合成医疗数据环境，包含两个公开可用的合成数据集，模拟了5万名接受心血管疾病一级预防的成年人数据，用于医学教育和研究方法开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私、治理和重新识别风险，患者级别的电子病历数据很少用于教学或方法开发，这限制了心血管风险建模的可重复性、透明度和实践培训。

Method: 基于用户指定的因果有向无环图，使用澳大利亚公开人口统计数据和已发表的流行病学效应估计进行参数化，生成合成数据（而非基于真实患者数据或生成模型）。

Result: 创建了两个合成数据资产：数据资产1提供干净、分析就绪的队列数据；数据资产2将同一队列重构为具有现实结构和词汇异质性的关系型EMR风格数据库。

Conclusion: PRIME-CVD能够在保护敏感信息的同时，支持数据清洗、协调、因果推理和政策相关风险建模的教学，通过Creative Commons Attribution 4.0许可证发布以支持可重复研究和可扩展医学教育。

Abstract: In recent years, progress in medical informatics and machine learning has been accelerated by the availability of openly accessible benchmark datasets. However, patient-level electronic medical record (EMR) data are rarely available for teaching or methodological development due to privacy, governance, and re-identification risks.
